Pipeline reference · OpenAlex 2026-01-16 · Dimensions June 2025 · PATSTAT 2023 Autumn In progress

What we measure, and how we know it works

Seven research pipelines cover papers on two bibliographic indices (OpenAlex and Dimensions), patents on two patent indices (PatentsView and PATSTAT), patent-to-paper citations, paper–patent pairs and court opinions. Explore their inputs, derived tables, metric definitions and validation figures. Coverage and computation choices are stated alongside the results.

Raw data

inputs · read-only

These source collections supply the seven pipelines. Authorship records provide native paper team sizes. SciSciNet is used for external validation only; it is not an input to the derived metrics. Source scales describe the available snapshots and need not equal the eligible sample in a figure.

SourceScaleAccessWhat it carries
Case law citation network
Science of Science/Case law		 5,179,698 opinions · 47,519,638 citation edges provided Edge_list.parquet and metadata.csv — US court opinions 1666–2020 with jurisdiction, court, reporter and decision date. Provided by the authors of Lee, S., Kim, T., Yoon, J., & Youn, H. (2024). When Common Law Ages: Two Centuries of Growing Inertia in US Judicial Opinions. arXiv preprint arXiv:2410.04493.
OpenAlex snapshot 2026-01-16
renli_shared/OpenAlex_2026_Jan_16_Renly_parquet		 ~396M works · ~2.2B reference edges read-only Works, references, locations, topics, concepts and IDs: 14 datasets under works/. This snapshot supplies the paper citation graph and metadata.
OpenAlex author–affiliation rows
works_au_affs_fixed.csv.gz		 40.8 GB gz · ~1.1B rows read-only One row per (work, author, affiliation). Distinct author IDs are aggregated into paper_author.parquet, the native source of team size in the current validation.
Dimensions June 2025 dump
jevans/dimensions/dimensions_june_2025		 155.5M publications · 2.14B reference edges read-only Digital Science BigQuery export: publications (one nested row per publication with references, authors and affiliations, ANZSRC FoR 2020 categories, source), source_titles and patents. Supplies the Dimensions paper citation graph, metadata, author lists and patent→paper channel.
PatentsView granted bulk
PatentView/Granted/*.tsv.zip		 35 files · 22 GB local Patents, applications, CPC, WIPO, disambiguated inventors and assignees, US patent and application citations.
PATSTAT Global 2023 Autumn
jevans/PATSTAT/unzipped_data		 128.0M applications · 507.5M citations read-only EPO worldwide bibliographic database: 65 zipped CSV parts, loaded once to zstd Parquet under PATSTAT/raw. tls201 applications, tls211 publications, tls212 citations with their origin, tls206/tls207 persons, tls209/tls224 IPC and CPC, tls230 WIPO fields. Supplies the patent citation graph, metadata and inventors three ways: applications on a filing clock and on a grant clock, and DOCDB families dated by priority year.
Reliance on Science
pcs/pcs_oa_uspto.csv		 34,825,379 links · 1.4 GB local 5,106,527 papers × 2,748,170 patents. Columns reftype, confscore, oaid, patent, wherefound; confidence 4–10.
Patent–paper pair list
PPP/_patent_paper_pairs_plus.csv		 548,315 pairs local 335,917 distinct papers · 309,729 distinct patents. The current trend outputs use the plus list, verified against the completed production log and Parquet row counts. Pair-list selection is configurable and must accompany comparisons across runs.
SciSciNet (comparison only)
OpenAlex/sciscinet_papers_fos_feg.parquet		 249,803,279 rows · 39 cols · 9.5 GB external External comparison only: metric-specific agreement in paper figure §3c. The current team-size figure uses native OpenAlex author IDs. Earlier restricted-run atypicality comparisons are identified separately in §9d.

Derived tables

91 Parquet files · 3 Oct 2026 UTC

Counts come from current Parquet footers; sizes come from the filesystem. Rows have different meanings across tables and must not be summed as unique documents. The inventory includes the legacy team-size table, the Dimensions 1990–2000 atypicality partition (the only one written so far) and the three PATSTAT sets (output/, output_grant/ and output_family/, same file names), but excludes backups, dated OpenAlex score partitions already represented by merged files, and sharded reference intermediates. Open a table's column list for each column's type, meaning and one example value from the table's first rows; columns that carry people's names show no example. How this inventory is refreshed.

Table / locationRowsSizeRow meaning / columns
paper_author.parquet
OpenAlex/output		251,654,5537.12 GBOne work_id with ordered, deduplicated author IDs and team_size.
5 columns · meaning and example
work_idstring
OpenAlex work id ('W' + digits, URL prefix stripped); the same values as paper_id in the sibling tables, under a different column name. One row per work with at least one author id in works_au_affs_fixed (251.7M works).e.g. W100000002
author_liststring
';'-joined OpenAlex author ids (opaque 'A' + digits) in byline order (author_position_int, ties broken by author id), each author listed once even when the source repeats them per affiliation.e.g. A5015414029;A5006412880
team_sizeint64
Number of distinct author ids on the work (= number of entries in author_list).e.g. 2
first_authorstring
OpenAlex author id ('A' + digits) at the lowest author position (first entry of author_list).e.g. A5015414029
last_authorstring
OpenAlex author id at the highest author position (last entry of author_list); equals first_author for single-author works.e.g. A5006412880
paper_author_country.parquet
OpenAlex/output		251,654,5531.58 GBOne work with the ISO2 countries of its authors' institutions: the sorted distinct set, the per-country author counts, and the first and last author's own countries.
9 columns · meaning and example
paper_idstring
OpenAlex work id as a string: 'W' followed by digits (e.g. W3002427681), URL prefix stripped; joins every other OpenAlex/output table. Same 251.7M works as paper_author (which calls the column work_id).e.g. W100000002
team_sizeint32
Number of distinct authors (author ids) on the work; identical to paper_author.team_size on every row.e.g. 2
n_locatedint32
Number of the work's authors with at least one country code (OpenAlex's per-authorship 'countries', i.e. the countries of the institutions their affiliations were matched to). 0 when no author is located.e.g. 2
countriesstring
Sorted, ';'-joined distinct ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 codes over all located authors (e.g. 'CN;US'); null when no author is located (55% of works). Namibia is 'NA', not a missing value. Coverage rises strongly over time, so take shares over located works.e.g. US
n_countriesint32
Number of distinct codes in countries; 0 when countries is null.e.g. 1
country_author_countsstring
Located authors per country as 'CC:n' items joined by ';', ordered by count descending then code (e.g. 'US:3;CN:1'). An author with two countries counts in both, so the counts can sum to more than n_located; null when no author is located.e.g. US:2
first_author_countrystring
';'-joined sorted country codes of the first author (lowest author_position_int, ties by numeric author id); can hold several codes. Null when that author has no country (not replaced by the next located author).e.g. US
last_author_countrystring
';'-joined sorted country codes of the last author (highest author_position_int); null when that author has no country.e.g. US
is_internationalbool
True when the work's authors span more than one country (n_countries > 1).e.g. false
paper_citation.parquet
OpenAlex/output		348,939,8372.38 GBOne document.
5 columns · meaning and example
paper_idstring
OpenAlex work id as a string: 'W' followed by digits (e.g. W3002427681), URL prefix stripped; joins every other OpenAlex/output table. One row per work with a usable publication year (348.9M), cited or not.e.g. W15
C_3int64
Number of citations received within 3 years of publication, publication year counted as year 0: incoming reference edges from OpenAlex works with 0 <= citing year - cited year <= 3 (calendar years), over the OpenAlex works/referenced_works graph (2026-01-16 snapshot), edges kept when both ends have a publication year in 1000-2030. 0 for uncited documents; cohorts less than 3 years before the snapshot are right-truncated.e.g. 0
C_5int64
Number of citations received within 5 years of publication, publication year counted as year 0: incoming reference edges from OpenAlex works with 0 <= citing year - cited year <= 5 (calendar years), over the OpenAlex works/referenced_works graph (2026-01-16 snapshot), edges kept when both ends have a publication year in 1000-2030. 0 for uncited documents; cohorts less than 5 years before the snapshot are right-truncated.e.g. 0
C_10int64
Number of citations received within 10 years of publication, publication year counted as year 0: incoming reference edges from OpenAlex works with 0 <= citing year - cited year <= 10 (calendar years), over the OpenAlex works/referenced_works graph (2026-01-16 snapshot), edges kept when both ends have a publication year in 1000-2030. 0 for uncited documents; cohorts less than 10 years before the snapshot are right-truncated.e.g. 0
C_allint64
Number of citations received from works published in or after the focal year (citing year - cited year >= 0, no upper bound), over the OpenAlex works/referenced_works graph (2026-01-16 snapshot), edges kept when both ends have a publication year in 1000-2030. Citations from works dated earlier than the focal document are dropped. 0 for uncited documents.e.g. 0
paper_citation_trend.parquet
OpenAlex/output		584,136,8592.22 GBOne document per citing year.
7 columns · meaning and example
paper_idstring
OpenAlex work id as a string: 'W' followed by digits (e.g. W3002427681), URL prefix stripped; joins every other OpenAlex/output table. Identifies the cited paper. Sparse panel: one row per (paper, citing year) with at least one citation of any channel; a missing year means zero, a never-cited paper is absent.e.g. W23
pub_yearint64
Publication year of the cited paper as dated in the citation graph (OpenAlex publication_year, kept when within 1000-2030; a few implausible pre-1800 years remain).e.g. 2012
cite_yearint64
Calendar year of the row's citations: the citing work's publication year for p2p, the citing patent's grant year (PatentsView g_patent patent_date) for pat2p_*. Rows with cite_year < pub_year are dropped; 2026 is a partial year.e.g. 2018
yrs_since_pubint64
cite_year - pub_year in years (>= 0; 0 = the publication year).e.g. 6
p2pint64
Number of OpenAlex works published in cite_year that cite this paper (incoming reference edges of the OpenAlex graph); 0 on rows that exist only for a patent citation.e.g. 1
pat2p_examinerint64
Number of US patent-to-paper citations (Reliance on Science / PCS links) whose reftype is 'exm' (examiner-added), dated by the citing patent's grant year. Effectively empty: only a few hundred such citations exist in the whole file, so use the sum of the two pat2p columns as total patent citations.e.g. 0
pat2p_non_examinerint64
Number of US patent-to-paper citations (Reliance on Science / PCS links) of any other reftype (overwhelmingly applicant 'app'), dated by grant year; only links whose patent number matches a PatentsView grant are counted, no confidence-score floor.e.g. 0
paper_disruption.parquet
OpenAlex/output		348,939,8376.38 GBOne document.
39 columns · meaning and example
paper_idstring
OpenAlex work id as a string: 'W' followed by digits (e.g. W3002427681), URL prefix stripped; joins every other OpenAlex/output table. One row per work with a usable publication year (348.9M), same rows as paper_citation.e.g. W7048716654
CD_3float
Funk & Owen-Smith CD (disruption) index for window '3': (ni - nj) / (ni + nj + nk), counting only papers with 0 <= year(citer) - year(focal) <= 3, publication year counted as year 0. Nominal range [-1, 1] (+1 disruptive, -1 consolidating). Null when the paper has no citers at all, or when neither a citer nor a paper citing its references falls in the window; 0 (not null) when the window has no citer but has papers citing the focal references. OpenAlex artefact: for roughly 0.1M papers per window that list themselves among their own references (self-citation edges), nk comes out negative, so CD can fall below -1 (minimum -2; about 30k papers for CD_all) or be null despite defined counts (about 80k for CD_all).e.g. 1
F_3float
Foundation share (0-1) for window '3': fraction of the focal paper's in-window citers whose own references include more of the focal paper's other in-window citers than of its references (down > up); ties with up = down > 0 count 1/2 here and 1/2 in E. F + E + G = 1. Null when the window has no citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
E_3float
Extension share (0-1) for window '3': fraction of in-window citers that cite more of the focal paper's references than of its other in-window citers (up > down), plus half of the ties. Null when the window has no citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
G_3float
Generalization share (0-1) for window '3': fraction of in-window citers that cite neither any of the focal paper's references nor any of its other in-window citers (up = down = 0). Null when the window has no citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 1
ni_3int32
Count of citers in window '3' (0 <= year(citer) - year(focal) <= 3, publication year counted as year 0) that cite none of the focal paper's references. -1 is a sentinel: the paper has no citers at all, or the window holds neither a citer nor a paper citing its references.e.g. -1
nj_3int32
Count of citers in window '3' that also cite at least one of the focal paper's references. -1 sentinel as for ni.e.g. -1
nk_3int32
Count of papers (other than the focal one) published in window '3' (0 <= year(citer) - year(focal) <= 3, publication year counted as year 0) that cite at least one of the focal paper's references but not the focal paper itself. -1 sentinel as for ni. OpenAlex artefact: about 0.1M papers have a computed nk of -1 (self-citation edges: the paper is in its own reference list), distinguishable from the sentinel because ni/nj are >= 0 there.e.g. -1
CD_5float
Funk & Owen-Smith CD (disruption) index for window '5': (ni - nj) / (ni + nj + nk), counting only papers with 0 <= year(citer) - year(focal) <= 5, publication year counted as year 0. Nominal range [-1, 1] (+1 disruptive, -1 consolidating). Null when the paper has no citers at all, or when neither a citer nor a paper citing its references falls in the window; 0 (not null) when the window has no citer but has papers citing the focal references. OpenAlex artefact: for roughly 0.1M papers per window that list themselves among their own references (self-citation edges), nk comes out negative, so CD can fall below -1 (minimum -2; about 30k papers for CD_all) or be null despite defined counts (about 80k for CD_all).e.g. 1
F_5float
Foundation share (0-1) for window '5': fraction of the focal paper's in-window citers whose own references include more of the focal paper's other in-window citers than of its references (down > up); ties with up = down > 0 count 1/2 here and 1/2 in E. F + E + G = 1. Null when the window has no citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
E_5float
Extension share (0-1) for window '5': fraction of in-window citers that cite more of the focal paper's references than of its other in-window citers (up > down), plus half of the ties. Null when the window has no citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
G_5float
Generalization share (0-1) for window '5': fraction of in-window citers that cite neither any of the focal paper's references nor any of its other in-window citers (up = down = 0). Null when the window has no citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 1
ni_5int32
Count of citers in window '5' (0 <= year(citer) - year(focal) <= 5, publication year counted as year 0) that cite none of the focal paper's references. -1 is a sentinel: the paper has no citers at all, or the window holds neither a citer nor a paper citing its references.e.g. -1
nj_5int32
Count of citers in window '5' that also cite at least one of the focal paper's references. -1 sentinel as for ni.e.g. -1
nk_5int32
Count of papers (other than the focal one) published in window '5' (0 <= year(citer) - year(focal) <= 5, publication year counted as year 0) that cite at least one of the focal paper's references but not the focal paper itself. -1 sentinel as for ni. OpenAlex artefact: about 0.1M papers have a computed nk of -1 (self-citation edges: the paper is in its own reference list), distinguishable from the sentinel because ni/nj are >= 0 there.e.g. -1
CD_10float
Funk & Owen-Smith CD (disruption) index for window '10': (ni - nj) / (ni + nj + nk), counting only papers with 0 <= year(citer) - year(focal) <= 10, publication year counted as year 0. Nominal range [-1, 1] (+1 disruptive, -1 consolidating). Null when the paper has no citers at all, or when neither a citer nor a paper citing its references falls in the window; 0 (not null) when the window has no citer but has papers citing the focal references. F + E + G = 1. Null when the window has no citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
E_10float
Extension share (0-1) for window '10': fraction of in-window citers that cite more of the focal paper's references than of its other in-window citers (up > down), plus half of the ties. Null when the window has no citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0

ni_10int32
Count of citers in window '10' (0 <= year(citer) - year(focal) <= 10, publication year counted as year 0) that cite none of the focal paper's references. -1 is a sentinel: the paper has no citers at all, or the window holds neither a citer nor a paper citing its references.e.g. -1

Count of citers in window '10' that also cite at least one of the focal paper's references. -1 sentinel as for ni.e.g. -1

Count of papers (other than the focal one) published in window '10' (0 <= year(citer) - year(focal) <= 10, publication year counted as year 0) that cite at least one of the focal paper's references but not the focal paper itself. -1 sentinel as for ni. OpenAlex artefact: about 0.1M papers have a computed nk of -1 (self-citation edges: the paper is in its own reference list), distinguishable from the sentinel because ni/nj are >= 0 there.e.g. -1
CD_allfloat
Funk & Owen-Smith CD (disruption) index for window 'all': (ni - nj) / (ni + nj + nk), counting only papers with year(citer) - year(focal) >= 0, no upper bound. Nominal range [-1, 1] (+1 disruptive, -1 consolidating). Null when the paper has no citers at all, or when neither a citer nor a paper citing its references falls in the window; 0 (not null) when the window has no citer but has papers citing the focal references. OpenAlex artefact: for roughly 0.1M papers per window that list themselves among their own references (self-citation edges), nk comes out negative, so CD can fall below -1 (minimum -2; about 30k papers for CD_all) or be null despite defined counts (about 80k for CD_all).e.g. 1
F_allfloat
Foundation share (0-1) for window 'all': fraction of the focal paper's in-window citers whose own references include more of the focal paper's other in-window citers than of its references (down > up); ties with up = down > 0 count 1/2 here and 1/2 in E. F + E + G = 1. Null when the window has no citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
E_allfloat
Extension share (0-1) for window 'all': fraction of in-window citers that cite more of the focal paper's references than of its other in-window citers (up > down), plus half of the ties. Null when the window has no citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
G_allfloat

ni_allint32
Count of citers in window 'all' (year(citer) - year(focal) >= 0, no upper bound) that cite none of the focal paper's references. -1 is a sentinel: the paper has no citers at all, or the window holds neither a citer nor a paper citing its references.e.g. 1
nj_allint32
Count of citers in window 'all' that also cite at least one of the focal paper's references. -1 sentinel as for ni.e.g. 0
nk_allint32
Count of papers (other than the focal one) published in window 'all' (year(citer) - year(focal) >= 0, no upper bound) that cite at least one of the focal paper's references but not the focal paper itself. -1 sentinel as for ni. OpenAlex artefact: about 0.1M papers have a computed nk of -1 (self-citation edges: the paper is in its own reference list), distinguishable from the sentinel because ni/nj are >= 0 there.e.g. 0
pctl_yeardouble
Cohort year for the CD percentiles: paper_metadata.year (publication year, stored as a double); present on every row.e.g. 1558
pctl_groupstring
Cohort field for the CD percentiles: the first field listed in paper_metadata.FoS_0, i.e. the OpenAlex field of the work's top-scoring topic (= FoS_rep). Null when the work has no topic (about 25%), and then every CD_*_pctl is null.e.g. Engineering
CD_3_pctldouble
Minimum-rank percentile (0-1] of CD_3 within its (pctl_year, pctl_group) cohort among papers with a non-null CD_3: rank() / n, ties share the lowest rank. Null when CD_3 or pctl_group is null. CD has large tie blocks (e.g. CD = 1), so use CD_3_pctl_cume for top-x% thresholds.e.g. 0.5
CD_3_pctl_cumedouble
Cumulative share (0-1] of the same cohort with CD_3 at or below this paper's value (ties inclusive, like cume_dist); always >= CD_3_pctl. The column to threshold for 'top x% most disruptive'. Null when CD_3 or pctl_group is null.e.g. 1
CD_5_pctldouble
Minimum-rank percentile (0-1] of CD_5 within its (pctl_year, pctl_group) cohort among papers with a non-null CD_5: rank() / n, ties share the lowest rank. Null when CD_5 or pctl_group is null. CD has large tie blocks (e.g. CD = 1), so use CD_5_pctl_cume for top-x% thresholds.e.g. 0.5
CD_5_pctl_cumedouble
Cumulative share (0-1] of the same cohort with CD_5 at or below this paper's value (ties inclusive, like cume_dist); always >= CD_5_pctl. The column to threshold for 'top x% most disruptive'. Null when CD_5 or pctl_group is null.e.g. 1
CD_10_pctldouble
Minimum-rank percentile (0-1] of CD_10 within its (pctl_year, pctl_group) cohort among papers with a non-null CD_10: rank() / n, ties share the lowest rank. Null when CD_10 or pctl_group is null. CD has large tie blocks (e.g. CD = 1), so use CD_10_pctl_cume for top-x% thresholds.e.g. 0.5
CD_10_pctl_cumedouble
Cumulative share (0-1] of the same cohort with CD_10 at or below this paper's value (ties inclusive, like cume_dist); always >= CD_10_pctl. The column to threshold for 'top x% most disruptive'. Null when CD_10 or pctl_group is null.e.g. 1
CD_all_pctldouble
Minimum-rank percentile (0-1] of CD_all within its (pctl_year, pctl_group) cohort among papers with a non-null CD_all: rank() / n, ties share the lowest rank. Null when CD_all or pctl_group is null. CD has large tie blocks (e.g. CD = 1), so use CD_all_pctl_cume for top-x% thresholds.e.g. 1
CD_all_pctl_cumedouble
Cumulative share (0-1] of the same cohort with CD_all at or below this paper's value (ties inclusive, like cume_dist); always >= CD_all_pctl. The column to threshold for 'top x% most disruptive'. Null when CD_all or pctl_group is null.e.g. 1
paper_hit_probability.parquet
OpenAlex/output		262,469,6283.19 GBOne document.
7 columns · meaning and example
paper_idstring
OpenAlex work id as a string: 'W' followed by digits (e.g. W3002427681), URL prefix stripped; joins every other OpenAlex/output table. Only works that have both a field (topic) and a publication year (262.5M).e.g. W18956409
FoSstring
Cohort field: OpenAlex field name (one of 26, e.g. 'Medicine') of the work's highest-scoring topic (paper_metadata.FoS_rep, else the first entry of FoS_0). Not comparable with Dimensions FoR divisions.e.g. Earth and Planetary Sciences
yearint64
Cohort year: the work's publication year (paper_metadata.year cast to integer; 1000-2030 in the file).e.g. 2013
pctl_c3float
Percentile (0-1] of the document's C_3 (paper_citation) within its (FoS, year) cohort: pandas rank(pct=True, method='min') = (number of cohort members with strictly fewer citations + 1) / cohort size. Uncited documents share the lowest value (1/cohort size); pctl >= 0.99 marks roughly the top 1%.e.g. 0.769895
pctl_c5float
Percentile (0-1] of the document's C_5 (paper_citation) within its (FoS, year) cohort: pandas rank(pct=True, method='min') = (number of cohort members with strictly fewer citations + 1) / cohort size. Uncited documents share the lowest value (1/cohort size); pctl >= 0.99 marks roughly the top 1%.e.g. 0.735841
pctl_c10float
Percentile (0-1] of the document's C_10 (paper_citation) within its (FoS, year) cohort: pandas rank(pct=True, method='min') = (number of cohort members with strictly fewer citations + 1) / cohort size. Uncited documents share the lowest value (1/cohort size); pctl >= 0.99 marks roughly the top 1%.e.g. 0.78191
pctl_callfloat
Percentile (0-1] of the document's C_all (paper_citation) within its (FoS, year) cohort: pandas rank(pct=True, method='min') = (number of cohort members with strictly fewer citations + 1) / cohort size. Uncited documents share the lowest value (1/cohort size); pctl >= 0.99 marks roughly the top 1%.e.g. 0.817987
paper_metadata.parquet
OpenAlex/output		372,655,60511.28 GBOne document.
18 columns · meaning and example
paper_idstring
OpenAlex work id as a string: 'W' followed by digits (e.g. W3002427681), URL prefix stripped; joins every other OpenAlex/output table. One row per work of works/works in the snapshot (372.7M), in snapshot order; one placeholder id 'W-1' is present.e.g. W3002427681
yeardouble
OpenAlex publication_year stored as float64 (null for 6.4% of works). Raw value, not range checked (1000-2050 in the file); the graph-based tables only keep works dated 1000-2030.e.g. 2020
doctypestring
OpenAlex work type (article, other, dataset, book-chapter, dissertation, preprint, book, review, paratext, letter, report, editorial, erratum, retraction, ...); not the MAG or Dimensions vocabulary.e.g. article
ref_countint32
Number of references the work lists in OpenAlex works/referenced_works (its rows in the referenced_works_w_year edge table), i.e. references to other OpenAlex works within this snapshot; 0 when none. Not comparable with SciSciNet or Dimensions counts.e.g. 44
journalstring
Display name (sources.csv.gz) of the work's primary-location source, or of its first location's source when there is no primary one. Any source type: journals, repositories, conferences, ebook platforms. Null when the work has no source (22%).e.g. Coatings
is_journalbool
True when that source's OpenAlex type is 'journal', False for other source types, null when the work has no source.e.g. true
author_listnull
Always null (Arrow type null): placeholder kept for schema compatibility; author lists are in paper_author.parquet.No non-null value in the first rows.
FoS_0string
';'-joined distinct OpenAlex field names (26 fields) of all the work's scored topics (up to 3, works/topics), ordered by topic score, best first; equals ';'.join(field). Null when the work has no topic (25%).e.g. Materials Science;Engineering;Environmental Science
FoS_repstring
OpenAlex field name of the work's highest-scoring topic (e.g. 'Materials Science'); the cohort key of paper_hit_probability. Null when no topic.e.g. Materials Science
domainstring
OpenAlex domain (Health Sciences, Life Sciences, Physical Sciences or Social Sciences) of the work's highest-scoring topic; null when no topic. No Dimensions twin.e.g. Physical Sciences
primary_topic_fieldstring
Field name of OpenAlex's own primary topic (works_semantic.primary_topic_json); null when the work has none. Equal to FoS_rep wherever both exist.e.g. Materials Science
primary_topic_subfieldstring
Subfield name (OpenAlex topic hierarchy, about 250 subfields) of the primary topic; null when none.e.g. Polymers and Plastics
primary_topic_topicstring
Display name of the primary topic (about 4,500 OpenAlex topics), looked up in topics.csv.gz by topic id; null when none.e.g. Flame retardant materials and properties
fieldlist<element: string>
List of the distinct field names of all the work's scored topics (1-3), best topic score first; null (not an empty list) when the work has no topic.e.g. [Materials Science, Engineering, Environmental Science]
subfieldlist<element: string>
List of the distinct subfield names of all the work's topics, best score first; de-duplicated by name (8 names are shared by two subfields and collapse to one entry); null when no topic.e.g. [Polymers and Plastics, Safety, Risk, Reliability and Q…, Health, Toxicology and Mutagene…]
topiclist<element: string>
List of all the work's topic names, one per scored topic (1-3), best score first; topic[0] equals primary_topic_topic. Null when no topic. Topic ids and scores are in paper_topics.parquet.e.g. [Flame retardant materials and p…, Fire dynamics and safety resear…, Toxic Organic Pollutants Impact]
cited_by_countint64
OpenAlex's own cited_by_count at snapshot time (all of OpenAlex); not the graph-derived, year-filtered C_all of paper_citation.e.g. 34
is_retractedbool
OpenAlex is_retracted flag; null for 0.3% of works.e.g. false
paper_sb.parquet
OpenAlex/output		88,314,669820.73 MBOne document.
4 columns · meaning and example
paper_idstring
OpenAlex work id as a string: 'W' followed by digits (e.g. W3002427681), URL prefix stripped; joins every other OpenAlex/output table. Only documents with at least one dated citation (citing year >= publication year) appear; uncited documents are omitted.e.g. W23
SB_Bfloat
Ke et al. (2015) beauty coefficient B from the yearly citation histogram C[t] (t = citing year - publication year >= 0, all years up to the snapshot): sum over t = 0..t_m of ((c_m - c_0)/t_m * t + c_0 - C[t]) / max(C[t], 1), where t_m is the age of the first citation peak. 0 when the peak is in the publication year; can be negative; higher = longer dormancy before a burst. Unitless; unreliable for small n_cite.e.g. 5
SB_Tint32
Awakening time: age in years since publication (not a calendar year), 0..t_m, at which the citation curve lies farthest from the straight line joining (0, C[0]) and the peak (t_m, C[t_m]). 0 when the paper peaks in its publication year.e.g. 5
n_citeint64
Number of dated citations the histogram was built from: citations from works published in or after the document's publication year, all years (>= 1).e.g. 5
paper_team_size.parquet
OpenAlex/output		251,654,5531.45 GBOne paper_id with a legacy team_size value.

The current project has no producer for this legacy file. Native team-size validation uses the reproducible paper_author.parquet output, whose team_size counts distinct authors rather than affiliation rows.

2 columns · meaning and example
paper_idstring
OpenAlex work id as a string: 'W' followed by digits (e.g. W3002427681), URL prefix stripped; joins every other OpenAlex/output table. Same 251.7M works as paper_author.parquet.e.g. W3021264700
team_sizeint32
Legacy team size: number of distinct OpenAlex author ids on the work. Its producing code is lost, but it equals paper_author.team_size on every work; superseded by paper_author.parquet.e.g. 5
paper_z_score.parquet
OpenAlex/output		41,425,0411.07 GBOne document.
5 columns · meaning and example
paper_idstring
OpenAlex work id as a string: 'W' followed by digits (e.g. W3002427681), URL prefix stripped; joins every other OpenAlex/output table. Only journal-type works (primary-location source of OpenAlex type 'journal') published 1980-2020, with 2-1000 dated references in the graph and at least one scorable journal pair (41.4M); merged from the year-range partitions, 1900-1979 never run.e.g. W100000241
Z_mediandouble
Median (numpy linear interpolation) of the Uzzi et al. (2013) z-scores of the distinct journal pairs formed by the journals of the paper's references (self-pairs included), each z taken from the paper's publication-year cohort (z_score_pair). Unitless; low or negative = atypical combinations, high = conventional.e.g. 39.4466
Z_10pctdouble
10th percentile (numpy linear interpolation) of the same distinct-pair z-scores: the novelty tail (negative = the paper makes some combinations rarer than chance).e.g. 8.11569
Z_mindouble
Minimum z over the paper's distinct journal pairs (an addition to Uzzi et al.).e.g. 4.42832
n_pairsint64
Number of distinct journal pairs among the paper's references that received a z (pairs whose null standard deviation is 0 are dropped); distinct pairs, not pair occurrences.e.g. 23
z_score_pair.parquet
OpenAlex/output		430,010,9332.53 GBOne journal pair per cohort year.
4 columns · meaning and example
code_1int64
Integer code of the first journal of the pair: the digits of the OpenAlex source id (S146344 -> 146344); only journal-type sources; code_1 <= code_2.e.g. 146344
code_2int64
Integer code of the second journal (the digits of the OpenAlex source id (S146344 -> 146344); only journal-type sources); equals code_1 for a self-pair (two references in the same journal).e.g. 146344
yearint64
Focal cohort year (1980-2020): publication year of the citing papers whose reference lists produced the pair; a pair's z is specific to this year.e.g. 1980
Z_scoredouble
z = (observed - null mean) / null SD for the journal pair in that year. Observed = co-occurrences of the pair across the reference lists of that year's focal papers, counted with multiplicity; null = 10 rewirings that swap cited works within each (citing year, cited year) cell; population SD (ddof=0); pairs with null SD 0 are omitted. Negative = rarer than chance (atypical).e.g. 1418.86
paper_author.parquet
Dimensions/output		142,431,5184.82 GBOne work_id with ordered, deduplicated author IDs and team_size.
9 columns · meaning and example
paper_idstring
Dimensions publication id: 'pub.' followed by digits (e.g. pub.1000000002); joins every other Dimensions/output table. One row per publication with at least one author slot (142.4M).e.g. pub.1000000002
author_liststring
';'-joined Dimensions researcher ids (opaque 'ur.' + digits ids) of the author slots Dimensions disambiguated, in byline order. Unresolved authors are skipped, so it has n_resolved entries, not team_size; null when no slot is resolved.e.g. ur.01241053402.31;ur.01042225174.03
team_sizeint32
Number of author slots in the publication's authors[] list, resolved or not. Unlike OpenAlex no de-duplication is needed (one entry per slot).e.g. 2
n_resolvedint32
Number of author slots carrying a Dimensions researcher_id (<= team_size).e.g. 2
first_authorstring
Researcher id of the first author slot; null when that slot is unresolved (not promoted to the next resolved author).e.g. ur.01241053402.31
last_authorstring
Researcher id of the last author slot; null when that slot is unresolved.e.g. ur.01042225174.03
corresponding_authorstring
Researcher id of the first author slot flagged corresponding; null when no slot is flagged or the flagged slot is unresolved (73% null).e.g. ur.01042225174.03
countriesstring
Sorted, ';'-joined distinct ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 codes over all authors' affiliation addresses (authors[].affiliations_address[].country_code); null when none. Identical to paper_author_country.countries.e.g. CA;HU
n_countriesint32
Number of distinct codes in countries; 0 when none.e.g. 2
paper_author_country.parquet
Dimensions/output		142,431,518763.04 MBOne work with the ISO2 countries of its authors' institutions: the sorted distinct set, the per-country author counts, and the first and last author's own countries.
9 columns · meaning and example
paper_idstring
Dimensions publication id: 'pub.' followed by digits (e.g. pub.1000000002); joins every other Dimensions/output table. Same 142.4M publications as paper_author (publications with an empty authors[] are absent).e.g. pub.1000000002
team_sizeint32
Number of author slots in authors[], resolved to a researcher id or not; identical to paper_author.team_size.e.g. 2
n_locatedint32
Number of author slots with at least one country code in their affiliation addresses; 0 when none.e.g. 2
countriesstring
Sorted, ';'-joined distinct ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 codes over all author slots' affiliations_address[].country_code (the affiliation's address country, whereas OpenAlex uses the matched institution's country); null when no slot is located. Namibia is 'NA'.e.g. CA;HU
n_countriesint32
Number of distinct codes in countries; 0 when countries is null.e.g. 2
country_author_countsstring
Located author slots per country as 'CC:n' items joined by ';', ordered by count descending then code (e.g. 'DE:2;SE:2'); a slot with two countries counts in both, so the sum can exceed n_located; null when no slot is located.e.g. CA:1;HU:1
first_author_countrystring
';'-joined sorted country codes of author slot 1 (can hold several); null when that slot has no country (not promoted to the next located author).e.g. HU
last_author_countrystring
';'-joined sorted country codes of the final author slot; null when that slot has no country.e.g. CA
is_internationalbool
True when n_countries > 1.e.g. true
paper_citation.parquet

paper_idstring
Dimensions publication id: 'pub.' followed by digits (e.g. pub.1000000002); joins every other Dimensions/output table. One row per publication with a year in 1700-2030 (155.4M), cited or not.e.g. pub.1000000001
C_3int64
Number of citations received within 3 years of publication, publication year counted as year 0: incoming reference edges from Dimensions works with 0 <= citing year - cited year <= 3 (calendar years), over the Dimensions reference graph (reference_ids of the June 2025 dump), edges kept when both ends have a year in 1700-2030. 0 for uncited documents; cohorts less than 3 years before the snapshot are right-truncated.e.g. 0
C_5int64
Number of citations received within 5 years of publication, publication year counted as year 0: incoming reference edges from Dimensions works with 0 <= citing year - cited year <= 5 (calendar years), over the Dimensions reference graph (reference_ids of the June 2025 dump), edges kept when both ends have a year in 1700-2030. 0 for uncited documents; cohorts less than 5 years before the snapshot are right-truncated.e.g. 0
C_10int64
Number of citations received within 10 years of publication, publication year counted as year 0: incoming reference edges from Dimensions works with 0 <= citing year - cited year <= 10 (calendar years), over the Dimensions reference graph (reference_ids of the June 2025 dump), edges kept when both ends have a year in 1700-2030. 0 for uncited documents; cohorts less than 10 years before the snapshot are right-truncated.e.g. 0
C_allint64

Citations from works dated earlier than the focal document are dropped. 0 for uncited documents.e.g. 0

paper_disruption.parquet
Dimensions/output		155,441,8565.02 GBOne document.
29 columns · meaning and example
paper_idstring
Dimensions publication id: 'pub.' followed by digits (e.g. pub.1000000002); joins every other Dimensions/output table. One row per publication with a year in 1700-2030 (155.4M), same rows as paper_citation.e.g. pub.1000000002
CD_3float
Funk & Owen-Smith CD (disruption) index for window '3': (ni - nj) / (ni + nj + nk), counting only papers with 0 <= year(citer) - year(focal) <= 3, publication year counted as year 0. Nominal range [-1, 1] (+1 disruptive, -1 consolidating). Null when the paper has no citers at all, or when neither a citer nor a paper citing its references falls in the window; 0 (not null) when the window has no citer but has papers citing the focal references.e.g. -0.046729
F_3float
Foundation share (0-1) for window '3': fraction of the focal paper's in-window citers whose own references include more of the focal paper's other in-window citers than of its references (down > up); ties with up = down > 0 count 1/2 here and 1/2 in E. F + E + G = 1. Null when the window has no citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0.178571
E_3float
Extension share (0-1) for window '3': fraction of in-window citers that cite more of the focal paper's references than of its other in-window citers (up > down), plus half of the ties. Null when the window has no citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0.75
G_3float
Generalization share (0-1) for window '3': fraction of in-window citers that cite neither any of the focal paper's references nor any of its other in-window citers (up = down = 0). Null when the window has no citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0.0714286
ni_3int32
Count of citers in window '3' (0 <= year(citer) - year(focal) <= 3, publication year counted as year 0) that cite none of the focal paper's references. -1 is a sentinel: the paper has no citers at all, or the window holds neither a citer nor a paper citing its references.e.g. 2
nj_3int32
Count of citers in window '3' that also cite at least one of the focal paper's references. -1 sentinel as for ni.e.g. 12
nk_3int32
Count of papers (other than the focal one) published in window '3' (0 <= year(citer) - year(focal) <= 3, publication year counted as year 0) that cite at least one of the focal paper's references but not the focal paper itself. -1 sentinel as for ni.e.g. 200

Nominal range [-1, 1] (+1 disruptive, -1 consolidating). Null when the paper has no citers at all, or when neither a citer nor a paper citing its references falls in the window; 0 (not null) when the window has no citer but has papers citing the focal references.e.g. -0.0422078

F + E + G = 1. Null when the window has no citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0.333333

paper_idstring

yearint32

doc_classstring
Dimensions document_type.classification, e.g. RESEARCH_ARTICLE, REVIEW_ARTICLE, CONFERENCE_PAPER, CONFERENCE_ABSTRACT, EDITORIAL, LETTER_TO_EDITOR, BOOK_REVIEW, CORRECTION_ERRATUM, REFERENCE_WORK, RESEARCH_CHAPTER, OTHER_*; null for 21%. Use it for a research-article-only filter. No OpenAlex twin.e.g. RESEARCH_ARTICLE
is_citablebool
Dimensions document_type.is_citable flag; null when doc_class is null.e.g. true
ref_countint32
Length of the publication's reference_ids list (Dimensions publication ids it cites, including ids absent from the dump); 0 when none. Not comparable with OpenAlex ref_count.e.g. 14
journalstring
Title (source_titles.title) of the publication's source (source_id, else journal.id). Any source type: journals, proceedings, book series, preprint platforms. Null when there is no source (15%).e.g. Rapid Communications in Mass Spectrometry
is_journalbool
True when the source's source_titles.type is 'journal'; False for other source types and for publications without a source (never null).e.g. true
source_idstring
Dimensions source id from the dump ('jour.' + digits for every source type); null when the dump has none.e.g. jour.1097836
author_liststring
';'-joined Dimensions researcher ids ('ur.' + digits) of the resolved author slots, in byline order. Empty string '' when the publication has authors but none is resolved (23% of rows), null when it has no author slots (8%); paper_author.parquet stores the empty case as null.e.g. ur.01241053402.31;ur.01042225174.03
team_sizeint32
Number of author slots in authors[], resolved or not; null when the publication has no author slots.e.g. 2
FoS_0string
';'-joined names of all ANZSRC Fields of Research 2020 divisions (2-digit level, categories.for_2020_v2022.first_level) assigned to the publication, in the dump's order; null when none (23%). Not OpenAlex fields.e.g. Chemical Sciences
FoS_repstring
Representative field: the first FoR 2020 division name listed (Dimensions assigns no score, so this is list order, not a best match); null when none. 22 distinct values in the file.e.g. Chemical Sciences
for_division_codesstring
';'-joined 2-digit FoR 2020 division codes (e.g. '30;40'), in the same order as FoS_0; null when none.e.g. 34
for_group_codesstring
';'-joined 4-digit FoR 2020 group codes (second level, e.g. '3005;4015'), in the dump's order; null when none (28%).e.g. 3401;3406
cited_by_countint64
Dimensions' own total citation count (metrics.times_cited) at export time, over the whole Dimensions index; not the graph-derived, year-filtered C_all of paper_citation.e.g. 52
citations_countint64
The dump's citations_count field (Dimensions' own count, like cited_by_count). Null rather than 0 for uncited publications (null for 46% of rows, minimum 1).e.g. 52
doistring
DOI as stored in the dump (no https://doi.org/ prefix); null for 5%.e.g. 10.1002/(sici)1097-0231(19991030)13:20<1999::aid-rcm744>3.0.co;2-c
paper_sb.parquet
Dimensions/output		83,849,678747.67 MBOne document.
4 columns · meaning and example
paper_idstring
Dimensions publication id: 'pub.' followed by digits (e.g. pub.1000000002); joins every other Dimensions/output table. Only documents with at least one dated citation (citing year >= publication year) appear; uncited documents are omitted.e.g. pub.1000000002
SB_Bfloat
Ke et al. (2015) beauty coefficient B from the yearly citation histogram C[t] (t = citing year - publication year >= 0, all years up to the snapshot): sum over t = 0..t_m of ((c_m - c_0)/t_m * t + c_0 - C[t]) / max(C[t], 1), where t_m is the age of the first citation peak. 0 when the peak is in the publication year; can be negative; higher = longer dormancy before a burst. Unitless; unreliable for small n_cite.e.g. 0
SB_Tint32
Awakening time: age in years since publication (not a calendar year), 0..t_m, at which the citation curve lies farthest from the straight line joining (0, C[0]) and the peak (t_m, C[t_m]). 0 when the paper peaks in its publication year.e.g. 0
n_citeint64
Number of dated citations the histogram was built from: citations from works published in or after the document's publication year, all years (>= 1).e.g. 52
paper_z_score_1990_2000.parquet
Dimensions/output		6,193,175184.58 MBOne publication with journal-pair atypicality scores; 1990-2000 partition only, no merged corpus-wide file yet.
5 columns · meaning and example
paper_idstring
Dimensions publication id: 'pub.' followed by digits (e.g. pub.1000000002); joins every other Dimensions/output table. Only journal-type publications (source_titles.type 'journal') published 1990-2000, with 2-1000 dated references in the graph and at least one scorable journal pair (6.2M). No other year range has been run.e.g. pub.1000000022
Z_mediandouble
Median (numpy linear interpolation) of the Uzzi et al. (2013) z-scores of the distinct journal pairs formed by the journals of the paper's references (self-pairs included), each z taken from the paper's publication-year cohort (z_score_pair). Unitless; low or negative = atypical combinations, high = conventional.e.g. 5.33833
Z_10pctdouble
10th percentile (numpy linear interpolation) of the same distinct-pair z-scores: the novelty tail (negative = the paper makes some combinations rarer than chance).e.g. -5.05889
Z_mindouble
Minimum z over the paper's distinct journal pairs (an addition to Uzzi et al.).e.g. -26.0656
n_pairsint64
Number of distinct journal pairs among the paper's references that received a z (pairs whose null standard deviation is 0 are dropped); distinct pairs, not pair occurrences.e.g. 439
z_score_pair_1990_2000.parquet
Dimensions/output		34,061,836297.52 MBOne journal pair per cohort year; 1990-2000 partition only.
4 columns · meaning and example
code_1int64
Integer code of the first journal of the pair: the digits of the Dimensions source id (jour.1013101 -> 1013101); only journal-type sources; code_1 <= code_2.e.g. 1013101
code_2int64
Integer code of the second journal (the digits of the Dimensions source id (jour.1013101 -> 1013101); only journal-type sources); equals code_1 for a self-pair (two references in the same journal).e.g. 1062273
yearint64
Focal cohort year (1990-2000): publication year of the citing papers whose reference lists produced the pair; a pair's z is specific to this year.e.g. 1990
Z_scoredouble
z = (observed - null mean) / null SD for the journal pair in that year. Observed = co-occurrences of the pair across the reference lists of that year's focal papers, counted with multiplicity; null = 10 rewirings that swap cited works within each (citing year, cited year) cell; population SD (ddof=0); pairs with null SD 0 are omitted. Negative = rarer than chance (atypical).e.g. 174.576
patent_citation.parquet
PatentView/output		7,160,531229.02 MBOne document.
45 columns · meaning and example
patent_idstring
USPTO patent number as a string, without 'US' prefix or kind code (7-8 digits, e.g. '10000000'; a few dozen 'RE...' ids that PatentsView types as utility). One row per utility patent that received at least one counted forward citation (granted or pre-grant-publication, lag >= 0); never-cited patents are absent rather than zero.e.g. 10000000
C_3int64
Forward citations to P from granted US utility patents (g_us_patent_citation) with 0 <= y_citing - y_P <= 3 (grant years, inclusive); counts citation rows (~0.16% duplicate rows count twice), third-party citations dropped; = C_examiner_3 + C_non_examiner_3 + C_unknown_3.e.g. 4
C_5int64
Forward citations to P from granted US utility patents (g_us_patent_citation) with 0 <= y_citing - y_P <= 5 (grant years, inclusive); counts citation rows (~0.16% duplicate rows count twice), third-party citations dropped; = C_examiner_5 + C_non_examiner_5 + C_unknown_5.e.g. 10
C_10int64
Forward citations to P from granted US utility patents (g_us_patent_citation) with 0 <= y_citing - y_P <= 10 (grant years, inclusive); counts citation rows (~0.16% duplicate rows count twice), third-party citations dropped; = C_examiner_10 + C_non_examiner_10 + C_unknown_10.e.g. 19
C_allint64
Forward citations to P from granted US utility patents (g_us_patent_citation) with y_citing >= y_P (grant years, any lag up to the 2025 snapshot end); counts citation rows (~0.16% duplicate rows count twice), third-party citations dropped; = C_examiner_all + C_non_examiner_all + C_unknown_all.e.g. 19
C_examiner_3int64
Part of C_3 whose citation_category is 'cited by examiner'. The flag is absent for citing patents granted up to 2001 (all of those fall in C_unknown_3), so examiner counts start with ~2002 citers.e.g. 2
C_non_examiner_3int64
Part of C_3 with a non-empty citation_category other than examiner or third party: 'cited by applicant', 'cited by other', 'imported from a related application' (mostly applicant references). Per the README the applicant/other labelling changed in 2013, but both stay in this bucket; zero for citers granted up to 2001.e.g. 2
C_unknown_3int64
Part of C_3 with an empty/NULL citation_category, kept separate rather than folded into non_examiner. In this snapshot that is every citation made by a patent granted 1976-2001 (and ~5% of 2002), so it is nonzero only through citers of that era.e.g. 0
C_examiner_5int64
Part of C_5 whose citation_category is 'cited by examiner'. The flag is absent for citing patents granted up to 2001 (all of those fall in C_unknown_5), so examiner counts start with ~2002 citers.e.g. 3
C_non_examiner_5int64
Part of C_5 with a non-empty citation_category other than examiner or third party: 'cited by applicant', 'cited by other', 'imported from a related application' (mostly applicant references). Per the README the applicant/other labelling changed in 2013, but both stay in this bucket; zero for citers granted up to 2001.e.g. 7
C_unknown_5int64
Part of C_5 with an empty/NULL citation_category, kept separate rather than folded into non_examiner. In this snapshot that is every citation made by a patent granted 1976-2001 (and ~5% of 2002), so it is nonzero only through citers of that era.e.g. 0

appC_3int64
Citations by granted US utility patents of P's pre-grant publication (g_us_application_citation; pgpub mapped to P via pg_granted_pgpubs_crosswalk) with 0 <= y_citing - y_P <= 3 (grant years, inclusive); rows, third party and negative-lag citations dropped; = appC_examiner_3 + appC_non_examiner_3.e.g. 7

uniqueC_3int64

patent_citation_trend.parquet
PatentView/output		50,546,229209.93 MBOne document per citing year.
15 columns · meaning and example
patent_idstring
USPTO patent number as a string, without 'US' prefix or kind code (7-8 digits, e.g. '10000000'; a few dozen 'RE...' ids that PatentsView types as utility). The cited patent; same 7,160,531 patents as patent_citation. Sparse: one row per (patent, citing year) with >= 1 citation, so a missing year means zero.e.g. 10000000
grant_yearint64
Grant year of the cited patent P (calendar year of g_patent.patent_date), 1976-2025.e.g. 2018
cite_yearint64
Grant year of the citing patents, used for both granted and pre-grant-publication citations (not the date the reference was made), 1976-2025.e.g. 2018
yrs_since_grantint64
cite_year - grant_year in years (0-49); citations with a negative lag are dropped. Summing a count over yrs_since_grant <= w reproduces patent_citation's _w column.e.g. 0
Cint64
Granted citations (g_us_patent_citation rows, third party dropped) received by P from patents granted in cite_year; per-year, not cumulative; = C_examiner + C_non_examiner + C_unknown.e.g. 0
C_examinerint64
Part of C with citation_category 'cited by examiner' (flag exists only for citers granted from ~2002).e.g. 0
C_non_examinerint64
Part of C with a non-empty category other than examiner/third party (applicant, other, imported from a related application).e.g. 0
C_unknownint64
Part of C with an empty citation_category: 100% of C for cite_year <= 2001, ~5% in 2002, 0 afterwards.e.g. 0
appCint64
Citations of P's pre-grant publication (pgpub mapped to P via pg_granted_pgpubs_crosswalk) made by patents granted in cite_year (g_us_application_citation, third party dropped); = appC_examiner + appC_non_examiner. Zero for every cite_year before 2005 in this build.e.g. 3
appC_examinerint64
Part of appC with citation_category 'cited by examiner'.e.g. 1
appC_non_examinerint64
Part of appC with any other category (no unknown bucket for application citations).e.g. 2
pat2pat_examinerint64
Legacy alias kept for older readers: identical to C_examiner.e.g. 0
pat2pat_non_examinerint64
Legacy alias: C_non_examiner + C_unknown (the old folded 'not examiner' definition), NOT equal to C_non_examiner.e.g. 0
app2pat_examinerint64
Legacy alias: identical to appC_examiner.e.g. 1
app2pat_non_examinerint64

patent_disruption.parquet
PatentView/output		8,062,166308.47 MBOne document.
39 columns · meaning and example
patent_idstring
USPTO patent number as a string, without 'US' prefix or kind code (7-8 digits, e.g. '10000000'; a few dozen 'RE...' ids that PatentsView types as utility). Utility patents granted 1976-2025 that appear in the granted citation graph (g_us_patent_citation, third-party rows not excluded) as citing or cited patent; never-cited patents have every metric null.e.g. 4859352
CD_3float
CD index (ni_3 - nj_3) / (ni_3 + nj_3 + nk_3) in [-1, 1] (+1 disruptive) on the granted utility-to-utility citation network (duplicate pairs and self-citations removed), counting citers and nk patents with 0 <= y - y_P <= 3 on grant years. Null if P is never cited or the denominator is 0; 0 with ni = nj = 0 when no citer is in the window but nk > 0. Inflated for early grant years (pre-1976 references missing).e.g. 0.230769
F_3float
Foundation share: fraction of P's citers in the window (0 <= y - y_P <= 3 on grant years) that cite more of P's other in-window citers than of P's references (down > up); a tie with up = down > 0 adds 0.5 to F and E. F + E + G = 1; null when P has no citer in the window. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
E_3float
Extension share: fraction of P's in-window citers that cite more of P's references than of P's other in-window citers (up > down), plus half of the up = down > 0 ties; null when no in-window citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
G_3float
Generalization share: fraction of P's in-window citers that cite none of P's references and none of P's other in-window citers; null when no in-window citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 1
ni_3float
Number of patents citing P in the window (0 <= y - y_P <= 3 on grant years) that cite none of P's references. Stored as float; null where CD_3 is null.e.g. 3
nj_3float
Number of patents citing P in the window that also cite at least one of P's references.e.g. 0
nk_3float
Number of patents granted in the window (0 <= y - y_P <= 3 on grant years) that cite at least one of P's references but not P.e.g. 10
CD_5float
CD index (ni_5 - nj_5) / (ni_5 + nj_5 + nk_5) in [-1, 1] (+1 disruptive) on the granted utility-to-utility citation network (duplicate pairs and self-citations removed), counting citers and nk patents with 0 <= y - y_P <= 5 on grant years. F + E + G = 1; null when P has no citer in the window. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0

Number of patents granted in the window (0 <= y - y_P <= 10 on grant years) that cite at least one of P's references but not P.e.g. 39
CD_allfloat
CD index (ni_all - nj_all) / (ni_all + nj_all + nk_all) in [-1, 1] (+1 disruptive) on the granted utility-to-utility citation network (duplicate pairs and self-citations removed), counting citers and nk patents with y - y_P >= 0 on grant years. Null if P is never cited or the denominator is 0; 0 with ni = nj = 0 when no citer is in the window but nk > 0. Inflated for early grant years (pre-1976 references missing).e.g. 0.245763
F_allfloat
Foundation share: fraction of P's citers in the window (y - y_P >= 0 on grant years) that cite more of P's other in-window citers than of P's references (down > up); a tie with up = down > 0 adds 0.5 to F and E. F + E + G = 1; null when P has no citer in the window. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0.280488
E_allfloat
Extension share: fraction of P's in-window citers that cite more of P's references than of P's other in-window citers (up > down), plus half of the up = down > 0 ties; null when no in-window citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0.134146
G_allfloat
Generalization share: fraction of P's in-window citers that cite none of P's references and none of P's other in-window citers; null when no in-window citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0.585366
ni_allfloat
Number of patents citing P in the window (y - y_P >= 0 on grant years) that cite none of P's references. Stored as float; null where CD_all is null.e.g. 35
nj_allfloat
Number of patents citing P in the window that also cite at least one of P's references.e.g. 6
nk_allfloat
Number of patents granted in the window (y - y_P >= 0 on grant years) that cite at least one of P's references but not P.e.g. 77
pctl_yeardouble
Cohort year of the CD percentile columns: P's filing year (patent_metadata.filing_year, earliest g_application filing date), stored as double; null for the few patents without one. The CD windows themselves run from the grant year.e.g. 1988
pctl_groupstring
Cohort group of the CD percentile columns: CPC Section letter A-H (Y allowed, none occur) = first character of P's primary CPC group patent_metadata.cpc_code; null (~17k patents) when there is no CPC code or no filing year.e.g. C
CD_3_pctldouble
Minimum-rank percentile of CD_3 within P's filing-year x CPC-Section cohort: (1 + # cohort patents with lower CD_3) / # with non-null CD_3, in (0, 1], higher = more disruptive; tie blocks (CD = 0, 1) share their lowest value. Null when CD_3 or pctl_group is null.e.g. 0.822563
CD_3_pctl_cumedouble
Cumulative percentile of CD_3 in the same cohort: share of cohort patents with CD_3 <= P's value (ties inclusive, like cume_dist); range (0, 1], always >= CD_3_pctl. The producer recommends this one for 'top x %' thresholds.e.g. 0.823973
CD_5_pctldouble
Minimum-rank percentile of CD_5 within P's filing-year x CPC-Section cohort: (1 + # cohort patents with lower CD_5) / # with non-null CD_5, in (0, 1], higher = more disruptive; tie blocks (CD = 0, 1) share their lowest value. Null when CD_5 or pctl_group is null.e.g. 0.686615
CD_5_pctl_cumedouble
Cumulative percentile of CD_5 in the same cohort: share of cohort patents with CD_5 <= P's value (ties inclusive, like cume_dist); range (0, 1], always >= CD_5_pctl. The producer recommends this one for 'top x %' thresholds.e.g. 0.687808
CD_10_pctldouble
Minimum-rank percentile of CD_10 within P's filing-year x CPC-Section cohort: (1 + # cohort patents with lower CD_10) / # with non-null CD_10, in (0, 1], higher = more disruptive; tie blocks (CD = 0, 1) share their lowest value. Null when CD_10 or pctl_group is null.e.g. 0.711367
CD_10_pctl_cumedouble
Cumulative percentile of CD_10 in the same cohort: share of cohort patents with CD_10 <= P's value (ties inclusive, like cume_dist); range (0, 1], always >= CD_10_pctl. The producer recommends this one for 'top x %' thresholds.e.g. 0.711444
CD_all_pctldouble
Minimum-rank percentile of CD_all within P's filing-year x CPC-Section cohort: (1 + # cohort patents with lower CD_all) / # with non-null CD_all, in (0, 1], higher = more disruptive; tie blocks (CD = 0, 1) share their lowest value. Null when CD_all or pctl_group is null.e.g. 0.694116

windowstring

n_base_rowsint64

Row count of patent_disruption_app.parquet (extended network), 8,460,168; the same on every row.e.g. 8460168
n_app_onlyint64
Patents present only in the extended table (reachable only via pre-grant-publication citations), 398,002; no patent is in the granted table only.e.g. 398002
n_pairedint64
Patents present in both tables with CD for this window non-null in both; every statistic below is over this set. Granted side = the pre-de-duplication build of patent_disruption (this ran 2026-09-02 before the dedup rebuild; base_mean matches the old file), which differs from the current one by < 1e-4 in mean CD.e.g. 5606555
base_meanfloat
Mean CD_w of the paired patents in the granted network (patent_disruption). Granted side = the pre-de-duplication build of patent_disruption (this ran 2026-09-02 before the dedup rebuild; base_mean matches the old file), which differs from the current one by < 1e-4 in mean CD.e.g. 0.0932952
app_meanfloat
Mean CD_w of the paired patents in the extended network (patent_disruption_app).e.g. 0.0631715
deltafloat
Mean of (extended - granted) CD_w over paired patents = app_mean - base_mean; negative means counting pre-grant-publication citations makes patents look more consolidating.e.g. -0.0301237
pearsondouble
Pearson correlation of CD_w, granted vs extended, over all paired patents.e.g. 0.775952
spearmandouble
Spearman rank correlation of CD_w, granted vs extended, estimated on a random sample of min(1,000,000, n_paired) paired patents (numpy seed 0).e.g. 0.810716
pct_downdouble
Percent (0-100) of paired patents whose CD_w is lower in the extended network.e.g. 23.1352
pct_updouble
Percent (0-100) of paired patents whose CD_w is higher in the extended network.e.g. 20.0828
pct_samedouble
Percent (0-100) of paired patents whose CD_w is exactly equal in both networks.e.g. 56.782
d_nifloat
Mean over paired patents of (extended - granted) ni_w: added citers that cite none of P's references.e.g. 0.898845

d_nkfloat

patent_inventor_country.parquet
PatentView/output		8,531,21636.53 MBOne utility patent with the ISO2 countries of the inventor addresses printed on the grant, the per-country inventor counts, and the assignee countries.
9 columns · meaning and example
patent_idstring
USPTO patent number as a string, without 'US' prefix or kind code (7-8 digits, e.g. '10000000'; a few dozen 'RE...' ids that PatentsView types as utility). Utility patents with >= 1 disambiguated inventor row (8,531,216 of 8,531,961).e.g. 10000000
n_inventorsint32
Distinct PatentsView-disambiguated inventors on the patent (de-duplicated on inventor_id, lowest sequence kept); equals the number of ids in patent_metadata.inventor_list.e.g. 1
n_locatedint32
Inventors whose location_id on this patent resolves to a country in g_location_disambiguated (<= n_inventors).e.g. 1
countriesstring
Distinct ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 codes of the located inventors, sorted and ';'-joined (e.g. 'DE;US'); the address printed on this grant, not the inventor's usual location. Null when no inventor is located. Namibia is 'NA'.e.g. US
n_countriesint32
Number of codes in countries; 0 when countries is null.e.g. 1
country_inventor_countsstring
Located inventors per country as 'CC:n' pairs, ';'-joined, ordered by count descending then code (e.g. 'US:3;JP:1'); counts sum to n_located; null when none is located.e.g. US:1
first_inventor_countrystring
ISO2 country of the first-listed (lowest inventor_sequence) inventor; null when that inventor is unlocated (the next located inventor is not substituted).e.g. US
is_internationalbool
True when n_countries > 1 (located inventors in two or more countries); False for 0 or 1.e.g. false
assignee_countriesstring
Distinct ISO2 codes of the patent's disambiguated assignees' locations on this grant, sorted and ';'-joined; null when the patent has no assignee or none is located.e.g. US
patent_metadata.parquet
PatentView/output		8,531,961868.26 MBOne document.
8 columns · meaning and example
patent_idstring
USPTO patent number as a string, without 'US' prefix or kind code (7-8 digits, e.g. '10000000'; a few dozen 'RE...' ids that PatentsView types as utility). Universe: g_patent rows with patent_type 'utility' and a parseable grant date (8,531,961).e.g. 10000000
grant_yearint64
Calendar year of the grant (issue) date g_patent.patent_date, 1976-2025.e.g. 2018
filing_yeardouble
Year of the earliest filing_date in g_application for the patent; double because nullable (null for 34 patents). A few source dates are implausible (11 before 1900, 46 after the grant year).e.g. 2015
ref_countint64
Backward references to US patents printed on the grant: number of g_us_patent_citation rows with this patent as citing patent and a non-null cited number, counting cited patents of any type, duplicate rows and third-party citations; 0 if none. Larger than the utility-only reference sets used by the citation/disruption tables.e.g. 2
cpc_codestring
Primary CPC classification: the cpc_group symbol (subclass + group/subgroup, e.g. 'G01S7/4863') of the lowest cpc_sequence row in g_cpc_current, i.e. the current CPC of the snapshot, not the CPC at grant; null for ~0.2% without CPC.e.g. G01S7/4863
cpc_code_liststring
All distinct cpc_group symbols of the patent (inventive and additional), ordered by cpc_sequence and ';'-joined; the first element is cpc_code.e.g. G01S7/4863;G01S7/4865;G01S7/4914;G01S7/4917;G01S17/894
inventor_liststring
PatentsView disambiguated inventor_ids (g_inventor_disambiguated), distinct, in inventor_sequence order, ';'-joined. Most ids embed name fragments ('fl:jo_ln:marron-5'); others are 25-character hashes or UUIDs. A few dozen ids contain ';' themselves, so split only where the next id starts, or use n_inventors from patent_inventor_country.Example withheld: the values carry people’s names.
assignee_liststring
PatentsView disambiguated assignee_ids (UUIDs, e.g. 'a45783ae-9cec-49bc-bc2e-7b064aaa5907'), distinct, in assignee_sequence order, ';'-joined; null for ~9% of patents with no assignee. Opaque ids; an assignee can be an organisation or an individual person.e.g. a45783ae-9cec-49bc-bc2e-7b064aaa5907
patent_reference.parquet
PatentView/output		171,532,249778.19 MBOne distinct (citing_id, cited_id, type) reference; grant_id identifies the granted cited endpoint.

Granted and application references may share a grant_id. Deduplicate (citing_id, grant_id) when unique patent edges are required; cited_id preserves the original cited identifier.

4 columns · meaning and example
citing_idstring
Patent number (string, no prefix) of the granted US utility patent making the reference. Rows are distinct (citing_id, cited_id, type); third-party citations dropped, both ends utility, and no grant-year lag filter (rows where the cited patent was granted after the citing one are kept).e.g. 10753736
cited_idstring
The cited document as cited: a granted utility patent number when type = 'granted'; an 11-digit pre-grant publication number (e.g. '20150201176' = publication year + 7-digit serial) when type = 'application'. Join on grant_id, not on this.e.g. 10000000
typestring
'granted' = reference to a granted patent (g_us_patent_citation); 'application' = reference to a pre-grant publication (g_us_application_citation) that later issued as grant_id. A patent citing both P and P's publication has two rows sharing grant_id.e.g. granted
grant_idstring
Granted utility patent the cited document is (granted rows: = cited_id) or became (application rows: via pg_granted_pgpubs_crosswalk); the join key to the other patent tables. Never null in this build, because citations of publications that never issued (~25M) were dropped.e.g. 10000000
patent_sb.parquet
PatentView/output		6,803,03459.77 MBOne document.
4 columns · meaning and example
patent_idstring
USPTO patent number of a granted patent of ANY type: utility (digits), design 'D...', plant 'PP...', reissue 'RE...', SIR 'H...', defensive publication 'T...'; unlike the other tables not limited to utility. Only patents with >= 1 dated citation.e.g. 10000000
SB_Bfloat
Ke et al. (2015) beauty coefficient from P's yearly citation counts c_t (t = citing grant year - P's grant year >= 0): B = sum_{t=0..t_m} [((c_tm - c_0)/t_m) t + c_0 - c_t] / max(1, c_t), t_m = first year of the maximum. 0 when the peak is in year 0; can be negative; unitless.e.g. 3.71429
SB_Tint32
Awakening time in years since grant: the t <= t_m with the largest distance from the line through (0, c_0) and (t_m, c_tm); 0 when the peak is in year 0. Integer, 0-48.e.g. 5
n_citeint64
Citations the histogram was built from: g_us_patent_citation rows citing P from a granted patent of any type with a non-negative grant-year lag (duplicates and third-party rows included); >= 1.e.g. 19
patent_z_score.parquet
PatentView/output		4,160,85677.60 MBOne document.
5 columns · meaning and example
patent_idstring
USPTO patent number as a string, without 'US' prefix or kind code (7-8 digits, e.g. '10000000'). Utility patents (patent_metadata) with >= 2 distinct inventional CPC subclasses (g_cpc_current, cpc_type = 'inventional').e.g. 3930274
Z_mediandouble
Median over P's CPC-subclass pairs of the pair z-score at P's grant year. Kim et al. (2016) z = (o - mu) / sigma, mu = n_a n_b / N, sigma^2 = mu (1 - n_a/N) (N - n_b)/(N - 1), with N, n_a, n_b, o counted over all patents with >= 2 inventional subclasses granted up to and including the grant year; z < 0 = atypical, z set to 0 when sigma = 0; in standard deviations.e.g. 39.2327
Z_10pctdouble
10th percentile (numpy, linear interpolation) of P's pair z-scores; same z definition as Z_median.e.g. 39.2327
Z_mindouble
Minimum of P's pair z-scores: its most atypical subclass pairing.e.g. 39.2327
n_pairsint64
Number of subclass pairs scored, k(k-1)/2 for k distinct inventional subclasses (1-820).e.g. 1
z_score_pair.parquet
PatentView/output		981,66210.60 MBOne CPC-subclass pair per cohort year.
4 columns · meaning and example
code_1string
First CPC subclass of the pair (4-character inventional subclass, e.g. 'A43B'); alphabetically before code_2.e.g. B63B
code_2string
Second CPC subclass of the pair, alphabetically after code_1.e.g. B63H
yearint64
Grant year t at which the pair is scored (1976-2025); a row exists only if the pair occurs on at least one patent granted in t.e.g. 1976
Z_scoredouble
Pair z-score on the cumulative set through year t. Kim et al. (2016) z = (o - mu) / sigma, mu = n_a n_b / N, sigma^2 = mu (1 - n_a/N) (N - n_b)/(N - 1), with N, n_a, n_b, o counted over all patents with >= 2 inventional subclasses granted up to and including the grant year; z < 0 = atypical, z set to 0 when sigma = 0; in standard deviations.e.g. 39.2327
patstat_citation.parquet
PATSTAT/output		40,901,611467.14 MBOne cited application with citation rows (C) and distinct citing applications (uniqueC) per window and provenance bucket.

Filing clock: filing years at both ends, every application.

33 columns · meaning and example
appln_idint64
PATSTAT application id (tls201.appln_id, integer < 900,000,000); the cited application. Only applications with at least one counted citation are rows; an absent application received none (patstat_hit_probability fills the zeros).e.g. 1
C_3int64
Citation rows received from citing applications whose filing year is 0 to 3 years after this application's filing year (0 <= age <= 3, inclusive at both ends): every patstat_reference row with age >= 0 that is not replenished (third-party observations removed upstream). One citing application can add several rows (several of its publications, several publications of the target, several origins), so C >= uniqueC.e.g. 11
C_examiner_3int64
The 'examiner' part of C_3 (citn_origin SEA, ISR, SUP, PRS, EXA, FOP or CH2: search-report, examination, opposition-filing and PCT chapter II citations): citation rows within the same window whose bucket is examiner; C_3 = C_examiner_3 + C_applicant_3 + C_other_3.e.g. 6
C_applicant_3int64
The 'applicant' part of C_3 (citn_origin APP: references submitted by the applicant): citation rows within the same window whose bucket is applicant; C_3 = C_examiner_3 + C_applicant_3 + C_other_3.e.g. 5
C_other_3int64
The 'other' part of C_3 (citn_origin OPP, APL or blank: opposition-division and appeal citations): citation rows within the same window whose bucket is other; C_3 = C_examiner_3 + C_applicant_3 + C_other_3.e.g. 0
C_5int64
Citation rows received from citing applications whose filing year is 0 to 5 years after this application's filing year (0 <= age <= 5, inclusive at both ends): every patstat_reference row with age >= 0 that is not replenished (third-party observations removed upstream). One citing application can add several rows (several of its publications, several publications of the target, several origins), so C >= uniqueC.e.g. 14
C_examiner_5int64
The 'examiner' part of C_5 (citn_origin SEA, ISR, SUP, PRS, EXA, FOP or CH2: search-report, examination, opposition-filing and PCT chapter II citations): citation rows within the same window whose bucket is examiner; C_5 = C_examiner_5 + C_applicant_5 + C_other_5.e.g. 7

Filing clock: filing years at both ends, every application.

9 columns · meaning and example
appln_idint64
PATSTAT application id (tls201.appln_id, integer < 900,000,000); the cited application. One row per (application, citing year) with at least one counted citation.e.g. 1
filing_yearint64
Clock year of the cited application: the application's filing year (tls201.appln_filing_year, 1900-2023).e.g. 2000
cite_yearint64
Filing year of the citing applications counted in this row (the citing application's clock year, not the publication year of the citation).e.g. 2000
yrs_since_filingint32
Years since the cited application's filing year: cite_year - filing_year (integer, >= 0; ps.EDGE_WHERE drops negative ages).e.g. 0
Cint64
Citation rows (patstat_reference rows, not replenished) received from applications of this citing year; one citing application can add several rows. Summing over years gives C_all of patstat_citation.e.g. 3
C_examinerint64
The 'examiner' part of C in this year (citn_origin SEA, ISR, SUP, PRS, EXA, FOP or CH2: search-report, examination, opposition-filing and PCT chapter II citations); C = C_examiner + C_applicant + C_other.e.g. 1
C_applicantint64
The 'applicant' part of C in this year (citn_origin APP: references submitted by the applicant); C = C_examiner + C_applicant + C_other.e.g. 2
C_otherint64
The 'other' part of C in this year (citn_origin OPP, APL or blank: opposition-division and appeal citations); C = C_examiner + C_applicant + C_other.e.g. 0
uniqueCint64
Distinct citing applications in this citing year. A citer has one clock year, so the yearly values sum exactly to uniqueC_all of patstat_citation.e.g. 3
patstat_disruption.parquet
PATSTAT/output		54,330,2121.86 GBOne application in the citation graph with CD, F / E / G and ni / nj / nk per window, plus CD percentiles within filing year x CPC Section.

Filing clock: filing years at both ends, every application.

39 columns · meaning and example
appln_idint64
PATSTAT application id (tls201.appln_id, integer < 900,000,000); every node of the de-duplicated application citation graph (distinct citing -> cited pairs from patstat_reference with age >= 0, not replenished), as citer or cited. Nodes that are never cited have NULL CD/F/E/G and -1 in ni/nj/nk.e.g. 1
CD_3float
CD disruption index (ni_3 - nj_3) / (ni_3 + nj_3 + nk_3) over applications dated 0 to 3 years after the focal application's filing year (0 <= age <= 3); in [-1, 1]. NULL when the window holds neither a citer nor a citer of a reference (then ni/nj/nk = -1); 0 when it has no citer but its references do (ni = nj = 0, nk > 0).e.g. 0.478261
F_3float
Foundation share: fraction of the citers dated 0 to 3 years after the focal application's filing year (0 <= age <= 3) for which down > up, where up = how many of its references (the applications it cites with age >= 0) the citer also cites and down = how many of the focal's other citers in the same window it cites; ties with up = down > 0 count half F, half E. NULL when there is no citer in the window. F + E + G = 1. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
E_3float
Extension share: fraction of the citers dated 0 to 3 years after the focal application's filing year (0 <= age <= 3) with up > down (citer cites more of the focal's references than of its other in-window citers), plus half of the up = down > 0 ties. NULL when there is no citer in the window. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
G_3float
Generalization share: fraction of the citers dated 0 to 3 years after the focal application's filing year (0 <= age <= 3) with up = down = 0 (cite neither the focal's references nor its other in-window citers). NULL when there is no citer in the window. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 1
ni_3int32
Citers dated 0 to 3 years after the focal application's filing year (0 <= age <= 3) that cite none of its references (the applications it cites with age >= 0) (count); -1 is the sentinel for 'nothing in the window' (CD_3 NULL), not a count.e.g. 11
nj_3int32
Citers dated 0 to 3 years after the focal application's filing year (0 <= age <= 3) that also cite at least one of its references (the applications it cites with age >= 0) (count); -1 sentinel when nothing is in the window.e.g. 0
nk_3int32
Applications dated 0 to 3 years after the focal application's filing year (0 <= age <= 3) that cite at least one of the focal's references but not the focal itself (distinct citers of the references minus nj, focal excluded); -1 sentinel when nothing is in the window.e.g. 12
