pctl_yearint64
CD-percentile cohort year: the application's filing year (patstat_metadata.filing_year).e.g. 2000
pctl_groupstring
CD-percentile cohort group: the CPC Section letter (A-H or Y) of patstat_metadata.cpc_code; NULL when there is no CPC code, and then every CD_*_pctl column is NULL.e.g. G
CD_3_pctldouble
Minimum-rank percentile of CD_3 within its pctl_year x pctl_group cohort, among applications with a non-NULL CD_3: rank() / n on ascending CD, ties share the lowest rank; in (0, 1], higher = more disruptive. NULL when CD_3 or pctl_group is NULL.e.g. 0.828953
CD_3_pctl_cumedouble
Cumulative percentile of CD_3 in the same cohort: share of the cohort's non-NULL CD_3 values <= this value (ties inclusive); in (0, 1], always >= CD_3_pctl, and differs from it inside CD's large tie blocks (e.g. many CD = 1 or 0). NULL when CD_3 or pctl_group is NULL.e.g. 0.828972
CD_5_pctldouble

patstat_disruption_trend.parquet
PATSTAT/output		349,993,8932.63 GBOne application per year with new and cumulative ni / nj / nk, CD and F / E / G.

Filing clock: filing years at both ends, every application.

14 columns · meaning and example
appln_idint64
PATSTAT application id (tls201.appln_id, integer < 900,000,000); a focal application with at least one citer in the de-duplicated graph. One row per (application, year) in which ni_new, nj_new or nk_new is non-zero; years without a change have no row.e.g. 1
filing_yearint32
Clock year of the focal application: the application's filing year (tls201.appln_filing_year, 1900-2023).e.g. 2000
cite_yearint32
Calendar filing year of this row = filing_year + yrs_since_filing; the year in which the new citers / co-citers counted in *_new appeared (up to 2023).e.g. 2000
yrs_since_filingint32
Years since the focal application's filing year (>= 0); the row at age w (or the last row before it) carries the window-w values of patstat_disruption.e.g. 0
ni_newint32
Citers of the focal whose filing year is exactly this year and that cite none of its references.e.g. 3
nj_newint32
Citers of the focal whose filing year is exactly this year and that also cite at least one of its references.e.g. 0
nk_newint32
Applications of exactly this year that cite at least one of the focal's references but not the focal (focal excluded).e.g. 3
niint32
Cumulative ni from age 0 through this row's age; at age w it equals ni_w of patstat_disruption and the last row equals ni_all (checked on every focal application).e.g. 3
njint32
Cumulative nj from age 0 through this row's age (equals nj_w of patstat_disruption at age w).e.g. 0
nkint32
Cumulative nk from age 0 through this row's age (equals nk_w of patstat_disruption at age w).e.g. 3
CDfloat
Disruption index (ni - nj) / (ni + nj + nk) on the cumulative counts, i.e. CD with a window of exactly this age; in [-1, 1], never NaN on a written row.e.g. 0.5
Ffloat
Share of the citers that have arrived by this age classed Foundation at this age (down > up, with down counted among citers of age <= this age; up = down > 0 ties count half). NaN (a float NaN, not NULL) on rows before the first citer arrives. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
Efloat
Share of the citers arrived by this age classed Extension (up > down, plus half of the ties); NaN before the first citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
Gfloat
Share of the citers arrived by this age classed Generalization (up = down = 0); NaN before the first citer. F + E + G = 1 when defined. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 1
patstat_disruption_trend_summary.parquet
PATSTAT/output		7,319367.91 KBOne (cohort year, years since) cell with mean cumulative CD / F / E / G.

Filing clock: filing years at both ends, every application.

10 columns · meaning and example
filing_yearint64
Cohort year of the focal documents: the application's filing year (tls201.appln_filing_year, 1900-2023).e.g. 1900
yrs_since_filingint64
Years since the cohort's filing year (>= 0).e.g. 0
n_CDint64
Number of patstat_disruption_trend rows in this (filing_year, yrs_since_filing) cell with a finite CD. Trend rows exist only for years in which a focal application gained a new citer or co-citer, so this counts those applications, not every cohort member.e.g. 1
CD_meandouble
Mean cumulative CD over the n_CD trend rows of this (filing_year, yrs_since_filing) cell (only applications with a change in exactly this year; values are not carried forward for the others).e.g. 1
n_Fint64
Number of patstat_disruption_trend rows in this (filing_year, yrs_since_filing) cell with a finite F. Trend rows exist only for years in which a focal application gained a new citer or co-citer, so this counts those applications, not every cohort member.e.g. 1

patstat_inventor.parquet
PATSTAT/output		75,617,4061.39 GBOne application with inventors: the person_id list in sequence order, its length, first and last (person records, not disambiguated inventors).

Filing clock: filing years at both ends, every application.

5 columns · meaning and example
appln_idint64
PATSTAT application id (tls201.appln_id, integer < 900,000,000); one row per application with at least one inventor row in tls207.e.g. 1
inventor_liststring
Inventor PATSTAT person_ids as a ';'-joined string in invt_seq_nr order (ties by person_id): a named person once at its lowest sequence, an unnamed placeholder record (empty tls206.person_name, e.g. person_id 263) once per slot, so its id can repeat. Person records, not disambiguated inventors; identical to patstat_metadata.inventor_list.e.g. 2;3;4;5
n_inventorsint32
Number of inventor slots = length of inventor_list (distinct named persons plus each slot of an unnamed placeholder); team size under its PatentView name. Not tls201.nb_inventors.e.g. 4
first_inventorint64
person_id (int64) of the inventor at the lowest sequence position.e.g. 2
last_inventorint64
person_id (int64) of the inventor at the highest sequence position (equals first_inventor for a single inventor).e.g. 5
patstat_inventor_country.parquet
PATSTAT/output		75,617,406235.13 MBOne application with inventors: the ISO2 countries of its inventors and applicants.

Filing clock: filing years at both ends, every application.

9 columns · meaning and example
appln_idint64
PATSTAT application id (tls201.appln_id, integer < 900,000,000); one row per application with at least one inventor row (same rows as patstat_inventor).e.g. 1
n_inventorsint32
Inventor slots under the once-per-named-person / once-per-placeholder-slot rule; identical to patstat_inventor.n_inventors.e.g. 4
n_locatedint32
Inventor slots whose person record has a two-letter country code (tls206.person_ctry_code matching [A-Z]{2}; malformed codes read as unknown); <= n_inventors.e.g. 4
countriesstring
Distinct inventor country codes, sorted alphabetically, ';'-joined (e.g. 'AU;GB;US'); NULL if no inventor is located. Codes are the address country recorded on that application's person record.e.g. FI
n_countriesint32
Number of codes in countries; 0 when countries is NULL.e.g. 1
country_inventor_countsstring
Inventors per country as 'CC:n' pairs joined by ';', ordered by count descending then code (e.g. 'GB:4;AU:1;US:1'); counts sum to n_located; NULL if none located.e.g. FI:4
first_inventor_countrystring
Country of the lowest-sequence inventor; NULL when that inventor has no country (not replaced by the next located inventor).e.g. FI
is_internationalbool
TRUE when n_countries > 1 (inventors in at least two countries).e.g. false
applicant_countriesstring
Distinct two-letter country codes of the applicants (all tls207 rows with applt_seq_nr > 0), sorted, ';'-joined; NULL if no applicant is located; identical to patstat_metadata.applicant_ctry_list.e.g. FI

Two-letter code of the office the application was filed at (tls201.appln_auth), e.g. EP, US, CN, JP, or WO for a PCT international application.e.g. AT
appln_kindstring
Application kind code (tls201.appln_kind, trimmed): A = patent application (about 91 % of rows), W = PCT application in the international phase, T = filing that translates or validates an EP / PCT application at some offices; D and the rare remaining codes as defined in the PATSTAT Data Catalog.e.g. A
appln_nrstring
Application number assigned by the filing office, as stored in tls201.appln_nr (a string in DOCDB format, e.g. '515678'); read it together with appln_auth and appln_kind.e.g. 517168
filing_yearint64
Filing year of the application (tls201.appln_filing_year, 1900-2023). The clock year of every metric in this set.e.g. 1968
filing_datedate32[day]
Filing date of the application (tls201.appln_filing_date).e.g. 1968-05-29
priority_yearint64
Year of the application's earliest filing date (tls201.earliest_filing_year: earliest of its own filing, its PCT application, its Paris-convention priorities, technical relations and continuations, direct links only); NULL replaces PATSTAT's 9999 default. Equals filing_year when no earlier linked filing exists; can precede 1900 (min 1819) because the 1900 floor applies to filing years only.e.g. 1967
publn_yearint64
Year of the application's earliest publication (tls201.earliest_publn_year); NULL where PATSTAT has 9999 (no dated publication).e.g. 1971
grant_yearint64
Year of the application's first grant publication (min year of tls211 publications flagged publn_first_grant = 'Y'); NULL when there is none, including about 0.76 M applications flagged granted only through a legal event.e.g. 1971
grantedbool
PATSTAT's granted flag (tls201.granted = 'Y'): a first-grant publication or an IP-right-grant legal event exists; for WO applications, granted in at least one designated state. TRUE for about 54 %.e.g. true
docdb_family_idint64
DOCDB simple-family id (tls201.docdb_family_id): applications sharing exactly the same priorities; the key of the family set (PATSTAT/output_family/).e.g. 6986579
docdb_family_sizeint64
PATSTAT's count of applications in the DOCDB simple family (tls201.docdb_family_size), all members including those outside this universe; 1 = the only member.e.g. 12
inpadoc_family_idint64
INPADOC extended-family id (tls201.inpadoc_family_id): applications linked directly or indirectly through priorities, PCT routes, technical relations or continuations; an integer id passed through from PATSTAT.e.g. 727113
nb_citing_docdb_famint64
PATSTAT's own family-level forward-citation count (tls201.nb_citing_docdb_fam): distinct DOCDB families citing any publication or application of this application's family, taken as-is from PATSTAT (this pipeline applies no window, universe or provenance filter to it); not the same as C / uniqueC.e.g. 3
nb_applicantsint64
PATSTAT's count of applicants on the most recent publication carrying Latin-script names (tls201.nb_applicants); 0 when none does, so it can disagree with applicant_list.e.g. 1
nb_inventorsint64
PATSTAT's count of inventors on the most recent publication carrying Latin-script names (tls201.nb_inventors); 0 when none does, so it can disagree with inventor_list and patstat_inventor.n_inventors.e.g. 1
ref_countint32
Number of distinct cited applications for which this application is the citer in this set's patstat_reference (both ends in the universe, third-party and replenished rows excluded, self-citations dropped, NO age filter so later-filed references count); 0 if none.e.g. 0
npl_ref_countint32
Number of distinct non-patent-literature documents (tls212.cited_npl_publn_id <> '0') cited by any publication of the application, replenished rows excluded (third-party NPL not removed); 0 if none.e.g. 0
ipc_mainstring
Main IPC symbol (tls209 row with ipc_position = 'F', whitespace removed, e.g. 'C07D499/06'; alphabetically first if several); NULL if none.e.g. A61J1/10
cpc_codestring
One representative CPC symbol (tls224, whitespace removed, e.g. 'G06K7/0013'): the alphabetically first symbol in the same 4-character subclass as ipc_main, else the alphabetically first symbol; NULL if no CPC. Its first letter is the CD-percentile group.e.g. A61M5/162
cpc_code_liststring
All distinct CPC symbols of the application (whitespace removed), sorted, ';'-joined; NULL if no CPC.e.g. A61M5/162
cpc_subclass_liststring
Distinct 4-character CPC subclasses (e.g. 'A61P;C07D'), sorted, ';'-joined; the atypicality input; NULL if no CPC.e.g. A61M
techn_field_nrint64
WIPO technology field 1-35 (Schmoch 2008 concordance, tls230_appln_techn_field) with the largest weight, ties to the lowest number; NULL if PATSTAT assigns none. Field names in ps_common.WIPO_FIELD.e.g. 13
wipo_sectorstring
WIPO sector of techn_field_nr: Electrical engineering (fields 1-8), Instruments (9-13), Chemistry (14-24), Mechanical engineering (25-32), Other fields (33-35); NULL when techn_field_nr is NULL. The hit-probability cohort sector.e.g. Instruments
inventor_liststring
Inventor PATSTAT person_ids (tls207 rows with invt_seq_nr > 0) as a ';'-joined string in invt_seq_nr order (ties by person_id): a named person once at its lowest sequence, an unnamed placeholder record (empty tls206.person_name) once per slot, so its id can repeat. person_ids are per-record, not disambiguated inventors; NULL if no inventor.e.g. 12842791
applicant_liststring
Applicant PATSTAT person_ids (tls207 rows with applt_seq_nr > 0), ';'-joined in applt_seq_nr order with the same once-per-named-person rule; person and company records, not disambiguated; NULL if no applicant.e.g. 12842790
inventor_ctry_liststring
Distinct two-letter country codes of the inventors (tls206.person_ctry_code of each person record; only [A-Z]{2} codes count, malformed ones are read as unknown), sorted, ';'-joined; NULL if no inventor has one (common for CN and JP filings).e.g. DE
applicant_ctry_liststring
Distinct two-letter country codes of the applicants (tls206.person_ctry_code, [A-Z]{2} only), sorted, ';'-joined; NULL if none.e.g. DE
applicant_sectorstring
PATSTAT standardised-name sector (tls206.psn_sector) of the applicant at sequence number 1: COMPANY, INDIVIDUAL, UNIVERSITY, GOV NON-PROFIT, HOSPITAL, UNKNOWN or a space-joined combination (e.g. 'GOV NON-PROFIT UNIVERSITY'); NULL if missing or blank.e.g. COMPANY

patstat_sb.parquet
PATSTAT/output		40,901,611315.95 MBOne cited application with its beauty coefficient and awakening time.

Filing clock: filing years at both ends, every application.

4 columns · meaning and example
appln_idint64
PATSTAT application id (tls201.appln_id, integer < 900,000,000); a cited application with at least one counted citation (same rows as patstat_citation).e.g. 1
SB_Bfloat
Ke et al. (2015) beauty coefficient from the yearly histogram of citation rows (not distinct citing applications) by age 0 .. last cited age (age >= 0, not replenished): sum over t <= t_m of (line from (0, c_0) to the peak (t_m, c_m) minus c_t) / max(c_t, 1). 0 when the first maximum is at age 0 (also the value for a single-year histogram); can be negative (min about -5); float32.e.g. 0
SB_Tint32
Awakening time: the age t <= t_m (years since the application's filing year) at which the histogram lies farthest from the line joining (0, c_0) and the peak; 0 when the peak is at age 0.e.g. 0
n_citeint64
Total citation rows (not distinct citing applications) in the histogram, i.e. C_all of patstat_citation.e.g. 19

patstat_z_score.parquet
PATSTAT/output		34,273,274512.27 MBOne application with at least two CPC subclasses: atypicality z summaries.

Filing clock: filing years at both ends, every application.

5 columns · meaning and example
appln_idint64
PATSTAT application id (tls201.appln_id, integer < 900,000,000); a application with at least two distinct CPC subclasses (its distinct 4-character CPC subclasses (tls224, whitespace removed)).e.g. 2
Z_mediandouble
Median over the application's subclass pairs of the pair z-score (Kim et al. (2016) hypergeometric z of a CPC-subclass pair in the application's filing year, with counts cumulative over all applications with >= 2 subclasses up to and including that year; z < 0 = atypical pair).e.g. -32.4608
Z_10pctdouble
10th percentile (linear interpolation, DuckDB quantile_cont) of the application's pair z-scores; low values flag atypical combinations.e.g. -32.4608
Z_mindouble
Minimum pair z-score of the application (its most atypical subclass pair).e.g. -32.4608
n_pairsint64
Number of distinct CPC-subclass pairs scored = k(k-1)/2 for k distinct subclasses.e.g. 1
z_score_pair.parquet
PATSTAT/output		2,629,98322.73 MBOne CPC-subclass pair per cohort year.

Filing clock: filing years at both ends, every application.

4 columns · meaning and example
code_1string
First CPC subclass of the pair (4 characters, e.g. 'A01B'); always alphabetically lower than code_2.e.g. A01B
code_2string
Second CPC subclass of the pair (4 characters); alphabetically higher than code_1.e.g. A01C
yearint64
Year in which at least one application with >= 2 subclasses carries this pair, on this set's clock: the application's filing year (tls201.appln_filing_year, 1900-2023).e.g. 1900
Z_scoredouble
Hypergeometric z = (o - mu) / sqrt(var) with mu = n_a n_b / N and var = mu (1 - n_a / N) (N - n_b) / (N - 1), where N, n_a, n_b and o (co-occurrences) count applications with >= 2 subclasses cumulatively through this year inclusive; 0 when var <= 0. z < 0 = rarer than chance (atypical).e.g. 4.49964

patstat_disruption.parquet
PATSTAT/output_grant		28,949,7421.02 GBOne application in the citation graph with CD, F / E / G and ni / nj / nk per window, plus CD percentiles within filing year x CPC Section.

Grant clock: grant years at both ends, granted applications only.

39 columns · meaning and example
appln_idint64
PATSTAT application id (tls201.appln_id, integer < 900,000,000) of a granted application (grant year 1900-2023); every node of the de-duplicated application citation graph (distinct citing -> cited pairs from patstat_reference with age >= 0, not replenished), as citer or cited. Nodes that are never cited have NULL CD/F/E/G and -1 in ni/nj/nk.e.g. 1
CD_3float
CD disruption index (ni_3 - nj_3) / (ni_3 + nj_3 + nk_3) over applications dated 0 to 3 years after the focal application's grant year (0 <= age <= 3); in [-1, 1]. NULL when the window holds neither a citer nor a citer of a reference (then ni/nj/nk = -1); 0 when it has no citer but its references do (ni = nj = 0, nk > 0).e.g. 0.75
F_3float
Foundation share: fraction of the citers dated 0 to 3 years after the focal application's grant year (0 <= age <= 3) for which down > up, where up = how many of its references (the applications it cites with age >= 0) the citer also cites and down = how many of the focal's other citers in the same window it cites; ties with up = down > 0 count half F, half E. NULL when there is no citer in the window. F + E + G = 1. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
E_3float
Extension share: fraction of the citers dated 0 to 3 years after the focal application's grant year (0 <= age <= 3) with up > down (citer cites more of the focal's references than of its other in-window citers), plus half of the up = down > 0 ties. NULL when there is no citer in the window. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
G_3float
Generalization share: fraction of the citers dated 0 to 3 years after the focal application's grant year (0 <= age <= 3) with up = down = 0 (cite neither the focal's references nor its other in-window citers). NULL when there is no citer in the window. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 1
ni_3int32
Citers dated 0 to 3 years after the focal application's grant year (0 <= age <= 3) that cite none of its references (the applications it cites with age >= 0) (count); -1 is the sentinel for 'nothing in the window' (CD_3 NULL), not a count.e.g. 3
nj_3int32
Citers dated 0 to 3 years after the focal application's grant year (0 <= age <= 3) that also cite at least one of its references (the applications it cites with age >= 0) (count); -1 sentinel when nothing is in the window.e.g. 0
nk_3int32
Applications dated 0 to 3 years after the focal application's grant year (0 <= age <= 3) that cite at least one of the focal's references but not the focal itself (distinct citers of the references minus nj, focal excluded); -1 sentinel when nothing is in the window.e.g. 1
CD_5float
CD disruption index (ni_5 - nj_5) / (ni_5 + nj_5 + nk_5) over applications dated 0 to 5 years after the focal application's grant year (0 <= age <= 5); in [-1, 1]. NULL when the window holds neither a citer nor a citer of a reference (then ni/nj/nk = -1); 0 when it has no citer but its references do (ni = nj = 0, nk > 0).e.g. 0.8
F_5float
Foundation share: fraction of the citers dated 0 to 5 years after the focal application's grant year (0 <= age <= 5) for which down > up, where up = how many of its references (the applications it cites with age >= 0) the citer also cites and down = how many of the focal's other citers in the same window it cites; ties with up = down > 0 count half F, half E. NULL when there is no citer in the window. F + E + G = 1. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
E_5float
Extension share: fraction of the citers dated 0 to 5 years after the focal application's grant year (0 <= age <= 5) with up > down (citer cites more of the focal's references than of its other in-window citers), plus half of the up = down > 0 ties. NULL when there is no citer in the window. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
G_5float
Generalization share: fraction of the citers dated 0 to 5 years after the focal application's grant year (0 <= age <= 5) with up = down = 0 (cite neither the focal's references nor its other in-window citers). NULL when there is no citer in the window. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 1
ni_5int32

pctl_groupstring

CD_3_pctldouble
Minimum-rank percentile of CD_3 within its pctl_year x pctl_group cohort, among applications with a non-NULL CD_3: rank() / n on ascending CD, ties share the lowest rank; in (0, 1], higher = more disruptive. NULL when CD_3 or pctl_group is NULL.e.g. 0.895304
CD_3_pctl_cumedouble
Cumulative percentile of CD_3 in the same cohort: share of the cohort's non-NULL CD_3 values <= this value (ties inclusive); in (0, 1], always >= CD_3_pctl, and differs from it inside CD's large tie blocks (e.g. many CD = 1 or 0). NULL when CD_3 or pctl_group is NULL.e.g. 0.89634

patstat_disruption_trend.parquet
PATSTAT/output_grant		194,798,3951.49 GBOne application per year with new and cumulative ni / nj / nk, CD and F / E / G.

Grant clock: grant years at both ends, granted applications only.

14 columns · meaning and example
appln_idint64
PATSTAT application id (tls201.appln_id, integer < 900,000,000) of a granted application (grant year 1900-2023); a focal application with at least one citer in the de-duplicated graph. One row per (application, year) in which ni_new, nj_new or nk_new is non-zero; years without a change have no row.e.g. 1
grant_yearint32
Clock year of the focal application: the grant year, i.e. the year of the application's first publication flagged publn_first_grant = 'Y' (1900-2023).e.g. 2008
cite_yearint32
Calendar grant year of this row = grant_year + yrs_since_grant; the year in which the new citers / co-citers counted in *_new appeared (up to 2023).e.g. 2008
yrs_since_grantint32
Years since the focal application's grant year (>= 0); the row at age w (or the last row before it) carries the window-w values of patstat_disruption.e.g. 0
ni_newint32
Citers of the focal whose grant year is exactly this year and that cite none of its references.e.g. 2
nj_newint32
Citers of the focal whose grant year is exactly this year and that also cite at least one of its references.e.g. 0
nk_newint32
Applications of exactly this year that cite at least one of the focal's references but not the focal (focal excluded).e.g. 1
niint32
Cumulative ni from age 0 through this row's age; at age w it equals ni_w of patstat_disruption and the last row equals ni_all (checked on every focal application).e.g. 2
njint32
Cumulative nj from age 0 through this row's age (equals nj_w of patstat_disruption at age w).e.g. 0
nkint32
Cumulative nk from age 0 through this row's age (equals nk_w of patstat_disruption at age w).e.g. 1
CDfloat
Disruption index (ni - nj) / (ni + nj + nk) on the cumulative counts, i.e. CD with a window of exactly this age; in [-1, 1], never NaN on a written row.e.g. 0.666667
Ffloat
Share of the citers that have arrived by this age classed Foundation at this age (down > up, with down counted among citers of age <= this age; up = down > 0 ties count half). NaN (a float NaN, not NULL) on rows before the first citer arrives. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
Efloat
Share of the citers arrived by this age classed Extension (up > down, plus half of the ties); NaN before the first citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
Gfloat
Share of the citers arrived by this age classed Generalization (up = down = 0); NaN before the first citer. F + E + G = 1 when defined. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 1

Years since the cohort's grant year (>= 0).e.g. 5

Number of patstat_disruption_trend rows in this (grant_year, yrs_since_grant) cell with a finite CD. Trend rows exist only for years in which a focal application gained a new citer or co-citer, so this counts those applications, not every cohort member.e.g. 1
CD_meandouble
Mean cumulative CD over the n_CD trend rows of this (grant_year, yrs_since_grant) cell (only applications with a change in exactly this year; values are not carried forward for the others).e.g. 1
n_Fint64
Number of patstat_disruption_trend rows in this (grant_year, yrs_since_grant) cell with a finite F. Trend rows exist only for years in which a focal application gained a new citer or co-citer, so this counts those applications, not every cohort member.e.g. 1
F_meandouble

n_Eint64
Number of patstat_disruption_trend rows in this (grant_year, yrs_since_grant) cell with a finite E. Trend rows exist only for years in which a focal application gained a new citer or co-citer, so this counts those applications, not every cohort member.e.g. 1
E_meandouble
Mean cumulative E (Extension share) over the n_E trend rows of this (grant_year, yrs_since_grant) cell (only applications with a change in exactly this year; values are not carried forward for the others). NULL when n_E = 0. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
n_Gint64
Number of patstat_disruption_trend rows in this (grant_year, yrs_since_grant) cell with a finite G. Trend rows exist only for years in which a focal application gained a new citer or co-citer, so this counts those applications, not every cohort member.e.g. 1
G_meandouble

patstat_inventor.parquet
PATSTAT/output_grant		39,284,673711.81 MBOne application with inventors: the person_id list in sequence order, its length, first and last (person records, not disambiguated inventors).

Grant clock: grant years at both ends, granted applications only.

5 columns · meaning and example
appln_idint64
PATSTAT application id (tls201.appln_id, integer < 900,000,000) of a granted application (grant year 1900-2023); one row per application with at least one inventor row in tls207.e.g. 1
inventor_liststring
Inventor PATSTAT person_ids as a ';'-joined string in invt_seq_nr order (ties by person_id): a named person once at its lowest sequence, an unnamed placeholder record (empty tls206.person_name, e.g. person_id 263) once per slot, so its id can repeat. Person records, not disambiguated inventors; identical to patstat_metadata.inventor_list.e.g. 2;3;4;5
n_inventorsint32
Number of inventor slots = length of inventor_list (distinct named persons plus each slot of an unnamed placeholder); team size under its PatentView name. Not tls201.nb_inventors.e.g. 4
first_inventorint64
person_id (int64) of the inventor at the lowest sequence position.e.g. 2
last_inventorint64
person_id (int64) of the inventor at the highest sequence position (equals first_inventor for a single inventor).e.g. 5
patstat_inventor_country.parquet
PATSTAT/output_grant		39,284,673128.98 MBOne application with inventors: the ISO2 countries of its inventors and applicants.

Grant clock: grant years at both ends, granted applications only.

9 columns · meaning and example
appln_idint64
PATSTAT application id (tls201.appln_id, integer < 900,000,000) of a granted application (grant year 1900-2023); one row per application with at least one inventor row (same rows as patstat_inventor).e.g. 1
n_inventorsint32
Inventor slots under the once-per-named-person / once-per-placeholder-slot rule; identical to patstat_inventor.n_inventors.e.g. 4
n_locatedint32
Inventor slots whose person record has a two-letter country code (tls206.person_ctry_code matching [A-Z]{2}; malformed codes read as unknown); <= n_inventors.e.g. 4
countriesstring
Distinct inventor country codes, sorted alphabetically, ';'-joined (e.g. 'AU;GB;US'); NULL if no inventor is located. Codes are the address country recorded on that application's person record.e.g. FI
n_countriesint32
Number of codes in countries; 0 when countries is NULL.e.g. 1
country_inventor_countsstring
Inventors per country as 'CC:n' pairs joined by ';', ordered by count descending then code (e.g. 'GB:4;AU:1;US:1'); counts sum to n_located; NULL if none located.e.g. FI:4
first_inventor_countrystring
Country of the lowest-sequence inventor; NULL when that inventor has no country (not replaced by the next located inventor).e.g. FI
is_internationalbool
TRUE when n_countries > 1 (inventors in at least two countries).e.g. false
applicant_countriesstring
Distinct two-letter country codes of the applicants (all tls207 rows with applt_seq_nr > 0), sorted, ';'-joined; NULL if no applicant is located; identical to patstat_metadata.applicant_ctry_list.e.g. FI
patstat_metadata.parquet
PATSTAT/output_grant		44,819,9752.44 GBOne application (appln_id) in the universe: a patent of invention, a real application, filed 1900-2023, with its office, years, family, classification, WIPO sector and persons.

Grant clock: grant years at both ends, granted applications only.

29 columns · meaning and example
appln_idint64
PATSTAT application id (tls201.appln_id, integer < 900,000,000) of a granted application (grant year 1900-2023); primary key shared with every other table in this set (the grant-clock universe).e.g. 43789986
appln_authstring
Two-letter code of the office the application was filed at (tls201.appln_auth), e.g. EP, US, CN, JP, or WO for a PCT international application.e.g. SU
appln_kindstring
Application kind code (tls201.appln_kind, trimmed): A = patent application (about 91 % of rows), W = PCT application in the international phase, T = filing that translates or validates an EP / PCT application at some offices; D and the rare remaining codes as defined in the PATSTAT Data Catalog.e.g. A
appln_nrstring
Application number assigned by the filing office, as stored in tls201.appln_nr (a string in DOCDB format, e.g. '515678'); read it together with appln_auth and appln_kind.e.g. 1727369
filing_yearint64
Filing year of the application (tls201.appln_filing_year, 1900-2023). Not the clock here (this set dates by grant_year), but it is the CD-percentile cohort year (pctl_year) of patstat_disruption.e.g. 1971
filing_datedate32[day]
Filing date of the application (tls201.appln_filing_date).e.g. 1971-12-21
priority_yearint64
Year of the application's earliest filing date (tls201.earliest_filing_year: earliest of its own filing, its PCT application, its Paris-convention priorities, technical relations and continuations, direct links only); NULL replaces PATSTAT's 9999 default. Equals filing_year when no earlier linked filing exists; can precede 1900 (min 1819) because the 1900 floor applies to filing years only.e.g. 1971
publn_yearint64
Year of the application's earliest publication (tls201.earliest_publn_year); NULL where PATSTAT has 9999 (no dated publication).e.g. 1991
grant_yearint64
Year of the application's first grant publication (min year of tls211 publications flagged publn_first_grant = 'Y'); the clock year of every metric in this set, 1900-2023, never NULL here.e.g. 1991
grantedbool
PATSTAT's granted flag (tls201.granted = 'Y'); always TRUE in this set, which holds only applications with a grant publication.e.g. true
docdb_family_idint64
DOCDB simple-family id (tls201.docdb_family_id): applications sharing exactly the same priorities; the key of the family set (PATSTAT/output_family/).e.g. 20496923
docdb_family_sizeint64
PATSTAT's count of applications in the DOCDB simple family (tls201.docdb_family_size), all members including those outside this universe; 1 = the only member.e.g. 1
inpadoc_family_idint64
INPADOC extended-family id (tls201.inpadoc_family_id): applications linked directly or indirectly through priorities, PCT routes, technical relations or continuations; an integer id passed through from PATSTAT.e.g. 43789986
nb_citing_docdb_famint64
PATSTAT's own family-level forward-citation count (tls201.nb_citing_docdb_fam): distinct DOCDB families citing any publication or application of this application's family, taken as-is from PATSTAT (this pipeline applies no window, universe or provenance filter to it); not the same as C / uniqueC.e.g. 3
nb_applicantsint64
PATSTAT's count of applicants on the most recent publication carrying Latin-script names (tls201.nb_applicants); 0 when none does, so it can disagree with applicant_list.e.g. 1
nb_inventorsint64
PATSTAT's count of inventors on the most recent publication carrying Latin-script names (tls201.nb_inventors); 0 when none does, so it can disagree with inventor_list and patstat_inventor.n_inventors.e.g. 1
ref_countint32
Number of distinct cited applications for which this application is the citer in this set's patstat_reference (both ends in the grant-clock universe, third-party and replenished rows excluded, self-citations dropped, NO age filter so later-granted references count); 0 if none.e.g. 0
npl_ref_countint32
Number of distinct non-patent-literature documents (tls212.cited_npl_publn_id <> '0') cited by any publication of the application, replenished rows excluded (third-party NPL not removed); 0 if none.e.g. 0
ipc_mainstring
Main IPC symbol (tls209 row with ipc_position = 'F', whitespace removed, e.g. 'C07D499/06'; alphabetically first if several); NULL if none.e.g. A01G3/02
cpc_codestring
One representative CPC symbol (tls224, whitespace removed, e.g. 'G06K7/0013'): the alphabetically first symbol in the same 4-character subclass as ipc_main, else the alphabetically first symbol; NULL if no CPC. Its first letter is the CD-percentile group.e.g. B66B7/046
cpc_code_liststring
All distinct CPC symbols of the application (whitespace removed), sorted, ';'-joined; NULL if no CPC.e.g. B66B7/046
cpc_subclass_liststring
Distinct 4-character CPC subclasses (e.g. 'A61P;C07D'), sorted, ';'-joined; the atypicality input; NULL if no CPC.e.g. B66B
techn_field_nrint64
WIPO technology field 1-35 (Schmoch 2008 concordance, tls230_appln_techn_field) with the largest weight, ties to the lowest number; NULL if PATSTAT assigns none. Field names in ps_common.WIPO_FIELD.e.g. 29
wipo_sectorstring
WIPO sector of techn_field_nr: Electrical engineering (fields 1-8), Instruments (9-13), Chemistry (14-24), Mechanical engineering (25-32), Other fields (33-35); NULL when techn_field_nr is NULL. The hit-probability cohort sector.e.g. Mechanical engineering
inventor_liststring
Inventor PATSTAT person_ids (tls207 rows with invt_seq_nr > 0) as a ';'-joined string in invt_seq_nr order (ties by person_id): a named person once at its lowest sequence, an unnamed placeholder record (empty tls206.person_name) once per slot, so its id can repeat. person_ids are per-record, not disambiguated inventors; NULL if no inventor.e.g. 35293992
applicant_liststring
Applicant PATSTAT person_ids (tls207 rows with applt_seq_nr > 0), ';'-joined in applt_seq_nr order with the same once-per-named-person rule; person and company records, not disambiguated; NULL if no applicant.e.g. 35293991
inventor_ctry_liststring
Distinct two-letter country codes of the inventors (tls206.person_ctry_code of each person record; only [A-Z]{2} codes count, malformed ones are read as unknown), sorted, ';'-joined; NULL if no inventor has one (common for CN and JP filings).e.g. SU
applicant_ctry_liststring
Distinct two-letter country codes of the applicants (tls206.person_ctry_code, [A-Z]{2} only), sorted, ';'-joined; NULL if none.e.g. SU
applicant_sectorstring
PATSTAT standardised-name sector (tls206.psn_sector) of the applicant at sequence number 1: COMPANY, INDIVIDUAL, UNIVERSITY, GOV NON-PROFIT, HOSPITAL, UNKNOWN or a space-joined combination (e.g. 'GOV NON-PROFIT UNIVERSITY'); NULL if missing or blank.e.g. COMPANY
patstat_reference.parquet
PATSTAT/output_grant		244,099,2373.26 GBOne citation row resolved to (citing application, cited application), with both clock years, age, the recorded origin and its examiner / applicant / other bucket, and a replenished flag.

Grant clock: grant years at both ends, granted applications only.

12 columns · meaning and example
citing_idint64
appln_id of the citing application (in the grant-clock universe (granted, grant year 1900-2023)).e.g. 57950047
cited_idint64
appln_id of the cited application (in the grant-clock universe (granted, grant year 1900-2023)); never equal to citing_id (self-citations through another publication are dropped).e.g. 49831125
citing_publn_idint64
tls211 pat_publn_id of the citing publication on which the citation is recorded (e.g. the A1 search-report publication or the B1 grant of the citing application).e.g. 511885918
cited_publn_idint64
tls211 pat_publn_id of the cited publication (tls212.cited_pat_publn_id); 0 exactly when the citation names an application instead (resolved = 'appln').e.g. 279376217
citing_yearint64
Grant year of the citing application (year of its first publication flagged publn_first_grant = 'Y').e.g. 2019
cited_yearint64
Grant year of the cited application.e.g. 2011
ageint32
citing_year - cited_year in years. Can be negative (a search report citing a later-granted document); such rows are kept here, and every metric requires age >= 0 (ps.EDGE_WHERE).e.g. 8
citing_publn_yearint64
Year of the citing publication's date (tls211.publn_date), a publication-based alternative clock; 9999 = date unknown in PATSTAT (a few hundred rows).e.g. 2019
citn_originstring
PATSTAT citation origin (tls212.citn_origin, trimmed): APP applicant, SEA search report, ISR international search report, SUP supplementary search report, PRS USPTO pre-search, EXA examination, FOP filed opposition, CH2 PCT chapter II, OPP opposition division, APL appeal. Third-party observations (115, TPO) are excluded upstream.e.g. SEA
bucketstring
Provenance bucket of citn_origin (ps.bucket_sql): 'applicant' = APP; 'examiner' = SEA, ISR, SUP, PRS, EXA, FOP, CH2; 'other' = anything else (OPP, APL, blank).e.g. examiner
replenishedbool
TRUE when PATSTAT copied the citation onto the citing publication from another publication (tls212.citn_replenished <> 0: Euro-PCT EP publications filled with the citations of their WO international publication). Kept for completeness; excluded from every count by ps.EDGE_WHERE.e.g. false
resolvedstring
How the cited end was resolved: 'publn' = via tls212.cited_pat_publn_id -> tls211 -> appln_id; 'appln' = via tls212.cited_appln_id directly (about 0.15 % of rows in the filing set).e.g. publn
patstat_sb.parquet
PATSTAT/output_grant		21,181,671167.98 MBOne cited application with its beauty coefficient and awakening time.

Grant clock: grant years at both ends, granted applications only.

4 columns · meaning and example
appln_idint64
PATSTAT application id (tls201.appln_id, integer < 900,000,000) of a granted application (grant year 1900-2023); a cited application with at least one counted citation (same rows as patstat_citation).e.g. 1
SB_Bfloat
Ke et al. (2015) beauty coefficient from the yearly histogram of citation rows (not distinct citing applications) by age 0 .. last cited age (age >= 0, not replenished): sum over t <= t_m of (line from (0, c_0) to the peak (t_m, c_m) minus c_t) / max(c_t, 1). 0 when the first maximum is at age 0 (also the value for a single-year histogram); can be negative (min about -5); float32.e.g. 0
SB_Tint32
Awakening time: the age t <= t_m (years since the application's grant year) at which the histogram lies farthest from the line joining (0, c_0) and the peak; 0 when the peak is at age 0.e.g. 0
n_citeint64
Total citation rows (not distinct citing applications) in the histogram, i.e. C_all of patstat_citation.e.g. 6
patstat_uniqueC_trend.parquet
PATSTAT/output_grant		90,032,716236.74 MBOne cited application per year since filing (grant) with its new distinct citing applications.

Grant clock: grant years at both ends, granted applications only.

5 columns · meaning and example
appln_idint64
PATSTAT application id (tls201.appln_id, integer < 900,000,000) of a granted application (grant year 1900-2023); the cited application. One row per (application, year) with at least one new citing application.e.g. 1
grant_yearint32
Clock year of the cited application: the grant year, i.e. the year of the application's first publication flagged publn_first_grant = 'Y' (1900-2023).e.g. 2008
cite_yearint32
grant_year + yrs_since_grant: the grant year of the citing applications counted in this row.e.g. 2008
yrs_since_grantint32
Lag in years from the cited application's grant year to the citer's (the minimum over the pair's edges, which is the only lag because both ends have one clock year each); >= 0.e.g. 0
uniqueCint64
Number of distinct citing applications whose citation of this application falls at this lag. Cumulative sums over lags <= 3/5/10/all reproduce uniqueC_3/5/10/all of patstat_citation, and the column equals patstat_citation_trend.uniqueC row for row (both asserted in the notebook).e.g. 2
patstat_z_score.parquet
PATSTAT/output_grant		18,033,300302.68 MBOne application with at least two CPC subclasses: atypicality z summaries.

Grant clock: grant years at both ends, granted applications only.

5 columns · meaning and example
appln_idint64
PATSTAT application id (tls201.appln_id, integer < 900,000,000) of a granted application (grant year 1900-2023); a application with at least two distinct CPC subclasses (its distinct 4-character CPC subclasses (tls224, whitespace removed)).e.g. 2
Z_mediandouble
Median over the application's subclass pairs of the pair z-score (Kim et al. (2016) hypergeometric z of a CPC-subclass pair in the application's grant year, with counts cumulative over all applications with >= 2 subclasses up to and including that year; z < 0 = atypical pair).e.g. -38.1998
Z_10pctdouble
10th percentile (linear interpolation, DuckDB quantile_cont) of the application's pair z-scores; low values flag atypical combinations.e.g. -38.1998
Z_mindouble
Minimum pair z-score of the application (its most atypical subclass pair).e.g. -38.1998
n_pairsint64
Number of distinct CPC-subclass pairs scored = k(k-1)/2 for k distinct subclasses.e.g. 1
z_score_pair.parquet
PATSTAT/output_grant		2,483,21521.53 MBOne CPC-subclass pair per cohort year.

Grant clock: grant years at both ends, granted applications only.

4 columns · meaning and example
code_1string
First CPC subclass of the pair (4 characters, e.g. 'A01B'); always alphabetically lower than code_2.e.g. A01B
code_2string
Second CPC subclass of the pair (4 characters); alphabetically higher than code_1.e.g. A01D
yearint64
Year in which at least one application with >= 2 subclasses carries this pair, on this set's clock: the grant year, i.e. the year of the application's first publication flagged publn_first_grant = 'Y' (1900-2023).e.g. 1900
Z_scoredouble
Hypergeometric z = (o - mu) / sqrt(var) with mu = n_a n_b / N and var = mu (1 - n_a / N) (N - n_b) / (N - 1), where N, n_a, n_b and o (co-occurrences) count applications with >= 2 subclasses cumulatively through this year inclusive; 0 when var <= 0. z < 0 = rarer than chance (atypical).e.g. 5.9431
patstat_citation.parquet
PATSTAT/output_family		30,785,709350.64 MBOne cited application with citation rows (C) and distinct citing applications (uniqueC) per window and provenance bucket.

Family unit: one row per DOCDB family (key docdb_family_id), earliest priority year at both ends, distinct family-to-family citations; read 'application' in the row meaning as 'family'.

33 columns · meaning and example
docdb_family_idint64
DOCDB simple-family id (tls201.docdb_family_id, integer); the cited family. Only families with at least one counted family citation are rows; an absent family received none (patstat_hit_probability fills the zeros).e.g. 38
C_3int64
Family citation rows received from citing families whose priority year is 0 to 3 years after this family's priority year (0 <= age <= 3, inclusive at both ends). The family edge list has one row per (citing family, cited family, provenance bucket), so a family citing under two buckets counts twice; within-family and replenished citations are excluded. Use uniqueC_3 for distinct citing families.e.g. 1
C_examiner_3int64
The 'examiner' part of C_3 (citn_origin SEA, ISR, SUP, PRS, EXA, FOP or CH2: search-report, examination, opposition-filing and PCT chapter II citations): citing families with at least one examiner-bucket application citation within the window. One row per family and bucket, so it equals uniqueC_examiner_3 in this set; C_3 = C_examiner_3 + C_applicant_3 + C_other_3.e.g. 1

9 columns · meaning and example
docdb_family_idint64
DOCDB simple-family id (tls201.docdb_family_id, integer); the cited family. One row per (family, citing year) with at least one counted citation.e.g. 38
priority_yearint64
Clock year of the cited family: the family's earliest priority year (min over its universe members of tls201.earliest_filing_year, the filing year where that is 9999). A few precede 1900 (min 1819) because the 1900 floor applies to filing years only.e.g. 1959
cite_yearint64
Priority year of the citing DOCDB families counted in this row (the citing family's clock year, not the publication year of the citation).e.g. 1962
yrs_since_priorityint32
Years since the cited family's priority year: cite_year - priority_year (integer, >= 0; ps.EDGE_WHERE drops negative ages).e.g. 3
Cint64
Family citation rows received in this citing year: one row per (citing family, provenance bucket), so a family citing under two buckets counts twice; within-family and replenished citations excluded. Summing over years gives C_all of patstat_citation.e.g. 1
C_examinerint64
The 'examiner' part of C in this year (citn_origin SEA, ISR, SUP, PRS, EXA, FOP or CH2: search-report, examination, opposition-filing and PCT chapter II citations); C = C_examiner + C_applicant + C_other.e.g. 1
C_applicantint64
The 'applicant' part of C in this year (citn_origin APP: references submitted by the applicant); C = C_examiner + C_applicant + C_other.e.g. 0
C_otherint64
The 'other' part of C in this year (citn_origin OPP, APL or blank: opposition-division and appeal citations); C = C_examiner + C_applicant + C_other.e.g. 0
uniqueCint64
Distinct citing DOCDB families in this citing year. A citer has one clock year, so the yearly values sum exactly to uniqueC_all of patstat_citation.e.g. 1
patstat_disruption.parquet
PATSTAT/output_family		37,623,6771.38 GBOne application in the citation graph with CD, F / E / G and ni / nj / nk per window, plus CD percentiles within filing year x CPC Section.

Family unit: one row per DOCDB family (key docdb_family_id), earliest priority year at both ends, distinct family-to-family citations; read 'application' in the row meaning as 'family'.

39 columns · meaning and example
docdb_family_idint64
DOCDB simple-family id (tls201.docdb_family_id, integer); every node of the de-duplicated family citation graph (distinct citing family -> cited family pairs with age >= 0), as citer or cited. Nodes that are never cited have NULL CD/F/E/G and -1 in ni/nj/nk.e.g. 75
CD_3float
CD disruption index (ni_3 - nj_3) / (ni_3 + nj_3 + nk_3) over families dated 0 to 3 years after the focal family's priority year (0 <= age <= 3); in [-1, 1]. NULL when the window holds neither a citer nor a citer of a reference (then ni/nj/nk = -1); 0 when it has no citer but its references do (ni = nj = 0, nk > 0).e.g. 1
F_3float
Foundation share: fraction of the citers dated 0 to 3 years after the focal family's priority year (0 <= age <= 3) for which down > up, where up = how many of its references (the families it cites with age >= 0) the citer also cites and down = how many of the focal's other citers in the same window it cites; ties with up = down > 0 count half F, half E. NULL when there is no citer in the window. F + E + G = 1. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
E_3float
Extension share: fraction of the citers dated 0 to 3 years after the focal family's priority year (0 <= age <= 3) with up > down (citer cites more of the focal's references than of its other in-window citers), plus half of the up = down > 0 ties. NULL when there is no citer in the window. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
G_3float
Generalization share: fraction of the citers dated 0 to 3 years after the focal family's priority year (0 <= age <= 3) with up = down = 0 (cite neither the focal's references nor its other in-window citers). NULL when there is no citer in the window. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 1
ni_3int32
Citers dated 0 to 3 years after the focal family's priority year (0 <= age <= 3) that cite none of its references (the families it cites with age >= 0) (count); -1 is the sentinel for 'nothing in the window' (CD_3 NULL), not a count.e.g. 1
nj_3int32
Citers dated 0 to 3 years after the focal family's priority year (0 <= age <= 3) that also cite at least one of its references (the families it cites with age >= 0) (count); -1 sentinel when nothing is in the window.e.g. 0
nk_3int32
Families dated 0 to 3 years after the focal family's priority year (0 <= age <= 3) that cite at least one of the focal's references but not the focal itself (distinct citers of the references minus nj, focal excluded); -1 sentinel when nothing is in the window.e.g. 0

pctl_yearint64
CD-percentile cohort year: the filing year of the family's earliest-filed universe member (patstat_metadata.filing_year in this set), not the priority year.e.g. 1959
pctl_groupstring
CD-percentile cohort group: the CPC Section letter (A-H or Y) of patstat_metadata.cpc_code (from the earliest-filed member with a CPC code); NULL when there is no CPC code, and then every CD_*_pctl column is NULL.e.g. H
CD_3_pctldouble
Minimum-rank percentile of CD_3 within its pctl_year x pctl_group cohort, among families with a non-NULL CD_3: rank() / n on ascending CD, ties share the lowest rank; in (0, 1], higher = more disruptive. NULL when CD_3 or pctl_group is NULL.e.g. 0.878328
CD_3_pctl_cumedouble
Cumulative percentile of CD_3 in the same cohort: share of the cohort's non-NULL CD_3 values <= this value (ties inclusive); in (0, 1], always >= CD_3_pctl, and differs from it inside CD's large tie blocks (e.g. many CD = 1 or 0). NULL when CD_3 or pctl_group is NULL.e.g. 1
CD_5_pctldouble
Minimum-rank percentile of CD_5 within its pctl_year x pctl_group cohort, among families with a non-NULL CD_5: rank() / n on ascending CD, ties share the lowest rank; in (0, 1], higher = more disruptive. NULL when CD_5 or pctl_group is NULL.e.g. 0.842571
CD_5_pctl_cumedouble
Cumulative percentile of CD_5 in the same cohort: share of the cohort's non-NULL CD_5 values <= this value (ties inclusive); in (0, 1], always >= CD_5_pctl, and differs from it inside CD's large tie blocks (e.g. many CD = 1 or 0). NULL when CD_5 or pctl_group is NULL.e.g. 1
CD_10_pctldouble
Minimum-rank percentile of CD_10 within its pctl_year x pctl_group cohort, among families with a non-NULL CD_10: rank() / n on ascending CD, ties share the lowest rank; in (0, 1], higher = more disruptive. NULL when CD_10 or pctl_group is NULL.e.g. 0.807051
CD_10_pctl_cumedouble
Cumulative percentile of CD_10 in the same cohort: share of the cohort's non-NULL CD_10 values <= this value (ties inclusive); in (0, 1], always >= CD_10_pctl, and differs from it inside CD's large tie blocks (e.g. many CD = 1 or 0). NULL when CD_10 or pctl_group is NULL.e.g. 1
CD_all_pctldouble
Minimum-rank percentile of CD_all within its pctl_year x pctl_group cohort, among families with a non-NULL CD_all: rank() / n on ascending CD, ties share the lowest rank; in (0, 1], higher = more disruptive. NULL when CD_all or pctl_group is NULL.e.g. 0.682674

PATSTAT/output_family		272,955,6672.11 GBOne application per year with new and cumulative ni / nj / nk, CD and F / E / G.

Family unit: one row per DOCDB family (key docdb_family_id), earliest priority year at both ends, distinct family-to-family citations; read 'application' in the row meaning as 'family'.

14 columns · meaning and example
docdb_family_idint64
DOCDB simple-family id (tls201.docdb_family_id, integer); a focal family with at least one citer in the de-duplicated graph. One row per (family, year) in which ni_new, nj_new or nk_new is non-zero; years without a change have no row.e.g. 38
priority_yearint32
Clock year of the focal family: the family's earliest priority year (min over its universe members of tls201.earliest_filing_year, the filing year where that is 9999). A few precede 1900 (min 1819) because the 1900 floor applies to filing years only.e.g. 1959
cite_yearint32
Calendar priority year of this row = priority_year + yrs_since_priority; the year in which the new citers / co-citers counted in *_new appeared (up to 2023).e.g. 1962
yrs_since_priorityint32
Years since the focal family's priority year (>= 0); the row at age w (or the last row before it) carries the window-w values of patstat_disruption.e.g. 3
ni_newint32
Citers of the focal whose priority year is exactly this year and that cite none of its references.e.g. 1
nj_newint32
Citers of the focal whose priority year is exactly this year and that also cite at least one of its references.e.g. 0
nk_newint32
Families of exactly this year that cite at least one of the focal's references but not the focal (focal excluded).e.g. 0
niint32
Cumulative ni from age 0 through this row's age; at age w it equals ni_w of patstat_disruption and the last row equals ni_all (checked on every focal family).e.g. 1
njint32
Cumulative nj from age 0 through this row's age (equals nj_w of patstat_disruption at age w).e.g. 0
nkint32
Cumulative nk from age 0 through this row's age (equals nk_w of patstat_disruption at age w).e.g. 0
CDfloat
Disruption index (ni - nj) / (ni + nj + nk) on the cumulative counts, i.e. CD with a window of exactly this age; in [-1, 1], never NaN on a written row.e.g. 1
Ffloat
Share of the citers that have arrived by this age classed Foundation at this age (down > up, with down counted among citers of age <= this age; up = down > 0 ties count half). NaN (a float NaN, not NULL) on rows before the first citer arrives. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
Efloat
Share of the citers arrived by this age classed Extension (up > down, plus half of the ties); NaN before the first citer. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
Gfloat
Share of the citers arrived by this age classed Generalization (up = down = 0); NaN before the first citer. F + E + G = 1 when defined. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 1

Percentile of uniqueC_examiner_all (distinct citing DOCDB families citing through the examiner bucket at any age) within the wipo_sector x priority_year cohort: rank() / n with ties at the minimum rank (pandas rank method 'min'), ascending, families absent from patstat_citation counted as 0; in (0, 1], higher = more cited, top 1 % <=> >= 0.99 (float32).e.g. 0.4674
pctl_uniqueC_applicant_allfloat

Ke et al. (2015) beauty coefficient from the yearly histogram of family citation rows (one per citing family and bucket) by age 0 .. last cited age (age >= 0, not replenished): sum over t <= t_m of (line from (0, c_0) to the peak (t_m, c_m) minus c_t) / max(c_t, 1). 0 when the first maximum is at age 0 (also the value for a single-year histogram); can be negative (min about -5); float32.e.g. 1

z_score_pair.parquet

4 columns · meaning and example
code_1string
First CPC subclass of the pair (4 characters, e.g. 'A01B'); always alphabetically lower than code_2.e.g. B01J
code_2string
Second CPC subclass of the pair (4 characters); alphabetically higher than code_1.e.g. C01B
yearint64
Year in which at least one family with >= 2 subclasses carries this pair, on this set's clock: the family's earliest priority year (min over its universe members of tls201.earliest_filing_year, the filing year where that is 9999). A few precede 1900 (min 1819) because the 1900 floor applies to filing years only.e.g. 1855
Z_scoredouble
Hypergeometric z = (o - mu) / sqrt(var) with mu = n_a n_b / N and var = mu (1 - n_a / N) (N - n_b) / (N - 1), where N, n_a, n_b and o (co-occurrences) count families with >= 2 subclasses cumulatively through this year inclusive; 0 when var <= 0. z < 0 = rarer than chance (atypical).e.g. 1

paper_idstring
OpenAlex work id of the cited paper, 'W' + the integer oaid of Reliance on Science (pcs_oa_uspto.csv). One row per paper with at least one US-patent citation in that file (C_total >= 1); papers never cited by a patent are absent, not zero.e.g. W979
C_3int64
Number of US patents citing the paper (Reliance on Science rows, any confidence score, front-page or in-text) whose PatentsView grant year y_q is 0 to 3 years after the paper's publication year (0 <= y_q - y_P <= 3, same year included), y_P = OpenAlex publication year. Citing documents without a PatentsView grant year (pre-grant publications, reissue/design/plant numbers) and papers without a year contribute 0; equals C_examiner_3 + C_non_examiner_3.e.g. 0
C_5int64
Number of US patents citing the paper (Reliance on Science rows, any confidence score, front-page or in-text) whose PatentsView grant year y_q is 0 to 5 years after the paper's publication year (0 <= y_q - y_P <= 5, same year included), y_P = OpenAlex publication year. C_10int64
Number of US patents citing the paper (Reliance on Science rows, any confidence score, front-page or in-text) whose PatentsView grant year y_q is 0 to 10 years after the paper's publication year (0 <= y_q - y_P <= 10, same year included), y_P = OpenAlex publication year. C_allint64

pcs_hit_probability.parquet
pcs/output		4,480,43479.47 MBOne document.
7 columns · meaning and example
paper_idstring
OpenAlex work id ('W' + integer). Rows are papers present in pcs_citation.parquet (i.e. cited by >= 1 US patent at any date) that also have a field and year in OpenAlex paper_metadata (4.48M); never-patent-cited papers are not in the cohorts.e.g. W2011473300
FoSstring
Cohort field: the OpenAlex topic-hierarchy field (one of 26, e.g. 'Medicine', 'Engineering') of the paper's highest-scoring topic (FoS_rep), else the first field of FoS_0; taken from OpenAlex paper_metadata at build time (2026-08-31).e.g. Medicine
yearint64
Cohort year: the paper's publication year from OpenAlex/output/paper_metadata.parquet (as of 2026-08-31).e.g. 1997
pctl_c3float
Percentile of the paper's patent-citation count C_3 (pcs_citation) within its (FoS, year) cohort, on a 0-1 scale: pandas rank(method='min', pct=True) = (1 + number of cohort papers with a strictly lower C_3) / cohort size, so values lie in (0, 1] and ties share the lowest rank. A paper with C_3 = 0 gets 1/cohort size, not 0; >= 0.99 reads as top 1 %, but only relative to other patent-cited papers.e.g. 3.6266e-05
pctl_c5float
Percentile of the paper's patent-citation count C_5 (pcs_citation) within its (FoS, year) cohort, on a 0-1 scale: pandas rank(method='min', pct=True) = (1 + number of cohort papers with a strictly lower C_5) / cohort size, so values lie in (0, 1] and ties share the lowest rank. A paper with C_5 = 0 gets 1/cohort size, not 0; >= 0.99 reads as top 1 %, but only relative to other patent-cited papers.e.g. 3.6266e-05
pctl_c10float
Percentile of the paper's patent-citation count C_10 (pcs_citation) within its (FoS, year) cohort, on a 0-1 scale: pandas rank(method='min', pct=True) = (1 + number of cohort papers with a strictly lower C_10) / cohort size, so values lie in (0, 1] and ties share the lowest rank. A paper with C_10 = 0 gets 1/cohort size, not 0; >= 0.99 reads as top 1 %, but only relative to other patent-cited papers.e.g. 0.478132
pctl_callfloat
Percentile of the paper's patent-citation count C_all (pcs_citation) within its (FoS, year) cohort, on a 0-1 scale: pandas rank(method='min', pct=True) = (1 + number of cohort papers with a strictly lower C_all) / cohort size, so values lie in (0, 1] and ties share the lowest rank. A paper with C_all = 0 gets 1/cohort size, not 0; >= 0.99 reads as top 1 %, but only relative to other patent-cited papers.e.g. 0.991006
ppp_paper_trend.parquet
PPP/output		5,788,07321.10 MBOne paper-patent linkage pair per citing year with paper-side counts.
8 columns · meaning and example
paperidstring
OpenAlex work id ('W' + integer) of the pair's paper; a PPP pairs a paper with the US patent judged to embody the same discovery, from _patent_paper_pairs_plus.csv (548,315 pairs over 335,917 papers and 309,729 patents, ppp_score 1-4; see PPP/ppprev1.pdf). Pairs whose paper is not in the OpenAlex citation graph (10.2 % of pairs) or that received no citations are absent.e.g. W2025049717
patentstring
The pair's US patent as written in the pair list: 'US-' + PatentsView patent number (e.g. 'US-10000036', reissues 'US-RE…', plant 'US-PP…'). The counts in this table belong to the paper, so they repeat identically for every pair that shares the paper.e.g. US-10000036
pub_yearint32
Publication year of the pair's paper from the OpenAlex citation graph cache (OpenAlex/cache/paper_csr.npz, works publication_year).e.g. 2014
cite_yearint64
Year of the citing documents: the citing work's OpenAlex publication year for p2p, and the citing US patent's PatentsView grant year for pat2p_*.e.g. 2015
yrs_since_pubint64
cite_year - pub_year in years, >= 0 (rows with a citing year before publication are dropped; same-year citations kept).e.g. 1
p2pint64
Paper->paper citations: number of OpenAlex works (any type, 2026-01-16 snapshot reference graph) published in cite_year that cite the pair's paper. Rows exist only for years with >= 1 p2p or pat2p citation, so 0 here means the row exists because of patent citations.e.g. 3
pat2p_examinerint64
Patent->paper citations to the pair's paper from US patents granted in cite_year whose Reliance on Science reftype is 'exm' (examiner). Near-empty (max 2); not usable for analysis.e.g. 0
pat2p_non_examinerint64
Patent->paper citations to the pair's paper from US patents granted in cite_year with any reftype other than 'exm' (applicant 'app'), from pcs/pcs_oa_uspto.csv; citing documents without a PatentsView grant year are excluded. Built from the same file and year sources as pcs_citation_trend.pcs_non_examiner, so per-year values should agree for the same paper.e.g. 0
ppp_patent_trend.parquet
PPP/output		2,906,16512.55 MBOne paper-patent linkage pair per citing year with patent-side counts.
7 columns · meaning and example
paperidstring
OpenAlex work id ('W' + integer) of the pair's paper (pair list _patent_paper_pairs_plus.csv: 548,315 paper-patent pairs judged to describe the same discovery; see PPP/ppprev1.pdf). It only identifies the pair: the counts belong to the patent and repeat for every pair sharing that patent.e.g. W2025049717
patentstring
The pair's US patent: 'US-' + PatentsView patent number (e.g. 'US-10000036', 'US-RE…'); the cited patent whose forward citations are counted.e.g. US-10000036
grant_yearint64
Grant year of the pair's patent (year of PatentsView g_patent patent_date); 1976-2023 in this table.e.g. 2018
cite_yearint64
Grant year of the citing US patents (PatentsView g_patent).e.g. 2022
yrs_since_grantint64
cite_year - grant_year in years, >= 0 (same-year citations kept, earlier ones dropped). Rows exist only for years with >= 1 counted citation.e.g. 4
pat2pat_examinerint64
Number of citation records in PatentsView g_us_patent_citation from patents granted in cite_year to the pair's patent with citation_category 'cited by examiner' (rows counted, not de-duplicated). PatentsView records examiner origin only for citing patents granted from 2001 on, so this is essentially 0 for earlier cite years.e.g. 0
pat2pat_non_examinerint64
Same count for every other citation_category ('cited by applicant', 'cited by other', and blank/NULL), with 'cited by third party' excluded. Because citations from patents granted up to 2000 have a blank category, all of them land here; this is not an applicant-only count.e.g. 1

C_3int32
Number of cases citing this case decided 0 to 3 years after it (0 <= y_c - y_P <= 3, same year included), by decision year of the citing case. The source GML is a simple directed graph, so this counts distinct citing cases.e.g. 0
C_5int32
Number of cases citing this case decided 0 to 5 years after it (0 <= y_c - y_P <= 5, same year included), by decision year of the citing case. The source GML is a simple directed graph, so this counts distinct citing cases.e.g. 0
C_10int32
Number of cases citing this case decided 0 to 10 years after it (0 <= y_c - y_P <= 10, same year included), by decision year of the citing case. The source GML is a simple directed graph, so this counts distinct citing cases.e.g. 0
C_allint32
Number of cases citing this case whose decision year is at or after its own (0 <= y_c - y_P, no upper bound). No edge in the graph has a negative lag, so this equals the case's in-degree; 0 for 1,391,837 never-cited cases.e.g. 3

case_disruption.parquet
Case law/output		5,179,698169.93 MBOne document.
29 columns · meaning and example
case_idint64
Caselaw Access Project (CAP) integer case id (metadata.csv id; values 1-12,707,012, 5,179,698 cases exist). One row per case.e.g. 24
CD_3float
CD disruption index (ni - nj) / (ni + nj + nk) over cases decided 0 to 3 years after the focal case (0 <= y - y_F <= 3); -1 consolidating to +1 disruptive. Null when the case is never cited at all, or when the window holds neither a citer nor an nk case; if the window has no citer but nk > 0 it is exactly 0 (not 'balanced').e.g. -0.0487805
F_3float
Foundation share (0-1) of the in-window citers c with e_i > e_j, ties e_i = e_j > 0 counted half; e_j = number of the focal case's references c also cites, e_i = number of the focal case's in-window citers c also cites. F + E + G = 1; null when the case has no citer in the window (C_3 = 0). Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
E_3float
Extension share (0-1) of the in-window citers with e_j > e_i (cites more of the focal case's references than of its citers), ties e_i = e_j > 0 counted half; null when C_3 = 0. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 1
G_3float
Generalization share (0-1) of the in-window citers with e_i = e_j = 0 (cites neither the focal case's references nor its other citers); null when C_3 = 0. Decomposition of Fang & Evans (2025), arXiv:2510.03240.e.g. 0
ni_3float
Count (stored as float) of cases decided 0 to 3 years after the focal case (0 <= y - y_F <= 3) that cite the focal case but none of its references. ni + nj equals case_citation.C_3; null exactly where CD_3 is null.e.g. 0
nj_3float
Count (float) of cases decided 0 to 3 years after the focal case (0 <= y - y_F <= 3) that cite the focal case and at least one of its references; null where CD_3 is null.e.g. 2
nk_3float

Decision year of the case (>= 1800 floor); second half of the cohort key.e.g. 1976

pctl_C_3double
Percentile of case_citation.C_3 within the (jurisdiction, decision_year) cohort on a 0-1 scale, DuckDB percent_rank = (rank - 1) / (cohort_n - 1) with ties at the lowest rank, i.e. the share of other cohort cases with strictly fewer citations. Uncited ties sit at 0, a unique maximum is 1, and a single-case cohort gives 0; >= 0.99 reads as top 1 %.e.g. 0

Percentile of case_citation.C_10 within the (jurisdiction, decision_year) cohort on a 0-1 scale, DuckDB percent_rank = (rank - 1) / (cohort_n - 1) with ties at the lowest rank, i.e. the share of other cohort cases with strictly fewer citations. Percentile of case_citation.C_all within the (jurisdiction, decision_year) cohort on a 0-1 scale, DuckDB percent_rank = (rank - 1) / (cohort_n - 1) with ties at the lowest rank, i.e. the share of other cohort cases with strictly fewer citations. decision_yearint32
Decision year: the leading four digits of CAP decision_date_original, kept if within 1600-2030, else null; populated for all cases (1666-2020). This is the time anchor of every Case law window.e.g. 1976

Raw CAP decision_date_original string, mostly 'YYYY-MM-DD'; about 7 % are not full dates (e.g. '1666-10'), kept so the year derivation is auditable.e.g. 1976-12-21
